## Tuning Knobs

*Every default is either a **[PLACEHOLDER: NAME]** with a registry candidate of that name or a locked value cited at the foot of the section by name. All knobs are data-driven (external config, never hard-coded in the render code). **★ marks a registry candidate whose value is a cross-system fact** (another GDD, the fixture or an authoring lint depends on it); unstarred knobs are internal to this system and stay in this table. Formula ids point at Formulas; rule ids at Detailed Design.*

### Growth, size and extent

| Knob | Default | Unit | Safe range | Too high | Too low | Interacts with |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| ★ `GROW_PX_PER_STEP[1]` | 2 | px per Graph step (20 px/s) | 1-4 | the sev-1 tell compresses: a 288 px room is crossed in under 7 s and the "dread with time to act" of ET-2 shortens | below 1 the 6-tile room is not crossed inside the 30 s ceiling (`PIPE:1081` item 15): invariant (i) breaks | D.2, FT-9, `W_MIN_WARNING_S` 10 (a "complete by 10 s" reading needs 3: tuning option, not a recommendation) |
| `GROW_PX_PER_STEP[2]` | 3 | px per step (30 px/s) | 2-8 | as above | below `GROW[1]` breaks monotonicity (iii) | D.2 |
| `GROW_PX_PER_STEP[3]` | 6 | px per step (60 px/s) | 3-7 | above 7 the cryo-bay front outruns the drip (72 px/s): "nothing moves faster than the drip" fails | below `GROW[2]` breaks (iii) | D.2 invariant (iv), `DRIP_FALL_PX_PER_FRAME`, `TRAIL_ANIM_FPS` |
| ★ `GROW_PX_PER_STEP[4]` | 15 | px per step (150 px/s) | 13-18 | above 18 (`V_RUN` 3 px/tick x 6) the breach front outruns a running player: no escape | 12 or below equals `V_WALK` x 6: the carried image of `ART:224` ("faster than you can walk") is false | `V_WALK` 2, `V_RUN` 3, FT-9 invariant (ii), the cascade front (FT-10) |
| `MIN_EXTENT_PX` | 48 | base px | 24-96 | a fresh fault already shows a long mark: the "it begins" read is lost | below 24 the source stamp is too small to find | D.2, `STAMP_PX[1]` |
| `SINCE_CLAMP` | 20000 | Graph steps (33 min) | 1000-65535 | shader-uniform overflow risk past 65535 | a long-lived trail stops growing before its edge ends | D.2, `DRIED_AFTER_STEPS` |
| ★ `TRAIL_EDGE_MAX_PX` | 1152 | base px (24 tiles) | 480-1536 | a Layout edge longer than the world cell budget; the stamp cap is reached sooner | Layout edges above it become content errors | D.2, D.3 whole-edge reach, Layout `anchor_geometry` (LY-23) |
| `TAPER_STAMPS` | 1 | run stamps per size tier | 1-3 | a long fat head: the "thickest at the source" gradient flattens | 1 is the floor of the range (the expression cannot taper faster) | D.1, FT-6 |
| `TRAIL_EXTENT_SMOOTH` | false | bool | - | on: the drawn extent lags the committed value by up to 0.1 s and is one more thing to test | off: extent steps 10 times a second, at most 15 px per step | D.2, FT-8, `KERN:298` (never extrapolate) |
| `DRIP_FALL_PX_PER_FRAME` | 6 | base px per frame (72 px/s at 12 fps) | 4-8 | a drip faster than the sev-3 front is fine; above 8 it reads as a stream | below 4 the front outruns it at sev 3 | D.2 invariant (iv), `GROW[3]`, `TRAIL_ANIM_FPS` |

### Frost, patches and puddles

| Knob | Default | Unit | Safe range | Too high | Too low | Interacts with |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| ★ `FROST_REACH_PX` (4 knots, warm to cold; and the step table) | 0 / 480 / 960 / 1152 at the four LS-7 edges | base px | monotone, 0-1152; **range floor: `frost_reach(T = -2000 mC)` >= 336 px (the temperature bound alone, at any `grow_steps` large enough to saturate the age bound)** | frost covers rooms that are merely cool: the P1 "colder means more" gradient flattens | below the floor the far-wall frost of `ART:142` / `LSG:839` is unreachable whatever `mode_since_step` the fixture authors | D.3, FT-11, Onboarding fixture, Graph LS-7 band edges (cited, not owned); bounds `frost_px` of a coolant leak (D.2d), not its extent *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review; user rulings)* |
| `FROST_REACH_INTERP` | true | bool | - | (true) none; continuous retreat | (false) frost retreat jumps up to 480 px at a band key; literal per-band table restored | D.3, CP-7 |
| `WET_SRC_PX` | 8 | base px (width of the widest wet-run piece) | 6-12, even | the wet run reads as a pipe or a puddle edge and not a trickle | below 6 the width law has fewer than three levels and a six-tile edge cannot be ordered by width | D.2d, FT-11, FT-22, FT-37 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* |
| `WET_MIN_PX` | 2 (fixed) | base px | fixed | - | below 2 breaks the 2 x 2 minimum feature (FT-22) | D.2d *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* |
| `WET_PITCH_PX` | 48 (fixed, = `STAMP_PX[1]`) | base px | fixed | - | - | D.2d, D.9 (the 12-cell worst case) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* |
| `PATCH_STRIDE` | 2 | tiles | 1-4 | sparse room frost | every tile frosted: a stamp-budget spike | D.2b, `PATCH_MAX_PER_ROOM` |
| `PATCH_MAX_PER_ROOM` | 24 | patches | 4-48 | stamp budget pressure: 12 rooms x 24 = 288 stamps is half the MVP worst case (D.9) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | a 42-tile bay shows too few patches to read as "the whole room is cold" | D.2b, `TRAIL_MAX_STAMPS` |
| `SOOT_RAMP_MC` | 20000 | mC above the SCORCH edge | 5000-40000 | soot spreads slowly across a room that is already burning equipment | the wash covers the room the step it starts | D.2b (`21 ATMO_HOT` sev 4) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| `ACC_STEP_LEN` (with `ACC_STEPS` 3 fixed) | 300 | Graph steps per accumulation step (30 s) | 100-1200 | scorch char accumulates too slowly to read as the tell of a warming box | char flecks jump in quickly | geometry class POINT (rows 2, 3, 7, 9), `grow_steps` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| `PUDDLE_ML_TIERS` (`PUDDLE_ML_M`, `PUDDLE_ML_L`) | 500 / 2000 | mL | Graph-unit dependent: re-tune after reading the Graph's `fuel_puddle_ml` unit | every puddle reads small | every puddle reads large | D.2c, Graph `LEAK_ML_MAX` (`LSG:925`), the body's "slows walking" rule |
| `ML_PER_PUDDLE_STAMP` | 250 | mL per stamp | 100-1000 | a big puddle is one stamp | stamp budget | D.2c |
| `PUDDLE_MAX_STAMPS` | 8 | stamps | 1-16 | budget | a flooded room under-reads | D.2c, `TRAIL_MAX_STAMPS` |

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `DENSITY_BUCKETS_PER_LEVEL` | 3 | 100-permille buckets | **2-3** (4 and 5 made density level 4 unreachable, because `bucket` tops out at 10) | most flows read as level 1 | levels change on nearly every key: noisy | D.4, `LSG:201` quantum, AC-167 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |

| `FRAMES_ACTIVE[1..4]` | 2 / 4 / 6 / 6 | frames | 1-6 | moving-medium cost | a vent looks static | D.4 |

| `TRAIL_ANIM_FPS` | 12 | frames per second | 8-12 (6-24 permitted) | costs little if shader-driven; arc `F` rises | mist looks like a slideshow; arc hold frames get long | D.8, D.8b, `PIPE:202` precedent |
| `N_FRAMES[medium]` | mist 6, drip 8, debris 6, dust 4, arc 6, shimmer 4 | frames | 2-12 | art cost | loop visible | D.6, D.8 |
| `TRAIL_VIS_SEED_FALLBACK` | 0 | int | any | - | - | D.6; used only if the kernel's read view of `kernel.clock` is unavailable |
| `N_VARIANTS` | 3 (s) / 2 (m, l) | variants per behaviour x size | 2-4 | art cost | visible repetition on a long chain | D.6, atlas pressure |
| `HOLE_MIN_BUCKET` | 5 | 100-permille bucket of a sev-4 `5 BREACH` `intensity_pm` at which the Void Steel hole replaces the bare bent rib (buckets 5-6, 7-8, 9-10 select the three authored holes) | 4-7 | holes appear late: a flowing breach still reads as a rib | a barely flowing plate shows a hole | row 5, D.6, AC-186 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* |

| `ARC_HOLD_FRAMES` | 3 | frames per flicker state | **3-6** (minimum raised from 2: hold 2 at 12 fps counts 4 flashes in a rolling window) | the arc reads as a slow pulse | 1 and 2 are forbidden (6 and 3-to-4 flashes/s) | D.8b, `TRAIL_ANIM_FPS`, knob invariant 5 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* |
| `ARC_FLICKER_PER_S` | 2 (**derived and read-only, not a knob**: ceil(floor(12 / 3) / 2), the ruled flash unit, D.8b) | flashes per second | not settable; the lint is on the rolling-window count `F_win` <= `FLASH_MAX_PER_S` | n/a | n/a | D.8b, `FLASH_MAX_PER_S` 3 (locked), `TRAIL_ANIM_FPS`, `ARC_HOLD_FRAMES` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* |

| `ARC_HELD_GLOW_PM` | 600 | permille of peak (the one hold level: reduce flashing and the reduced-motion arc pose) | 400-800 | the held arc reads as a lit lamp, not a fault | the held arc is invisible on a mid floor *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | D.8b, FT-28, FT-29 |
| `ARC_PER_REGION_MAX` | 2 | arcs per compartment, and per neighbourhood window (`NB_DX_PX` 272 x `NB_DY_PX` 216 base px, fixed, derived from `REGION_MIN_PX` plus one l cell) | 1-3 | the per-arc swing shrinks as 1/N (57 for three l arcs) and the pop becomes hard to see | - | D.8b (`n_arcs_room`, counted over the neighbourhood); content lint on live-capable SHORT anchors per compartment and per window *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review; re-review)* |

### Cited, not tuned here (the source of truth is another document)

Kernel: `TICK_RATE` 60, `GRAPH_STEP_TICKS` 6, `MAX_DIRTY_KEYS` 256, `STEP_BUDGET_MS` 4. Pipeline and Controller: `TILE_PX` 48, `WALL_FOOTPRINT_DEPTH` 24, `V_WALK` 2 and `V_RUN` 3 px per tick, `FLASH_MAX_PER_S` 3, the ladder `[1, k_auto + 2]`. Graph (registered): `HP_FULL` 10000, `HP_OK` 7000, `HP_FAIL` 3000, `W_MIN_WARNING_S` 10, `PATCH_CAP_BP` 6000, `PATCH_DECAY_S` 240, `MAX_FOOTPRINTS_PER_STEP` 16, `MAX_SPREAD_PER_STEP` 32, `HATCH_DT_FROST_MC` 20000, `WELD_DP_WARN` 300. Graph (registered, cited by name): `LEAK_HP` 4000, `BREACH_HP` 4000, `SEV_STEP` 1500, `A_HOLE_MAX` 64, `ALARM_SEV_MIN` 3, `OPEN_END_RATE_PM` 500, `FOOTPRINT_MAX_Q` 20000, `FOOTPRINT_MAX_GOUGE_BP` 2500, `FREEZE_WARN_MC` -10000, `FREEZE_ON_MC` -20000, `FREEZE_OFF_MC` -10000, `OVERHEAT_ON_MC` 70000, `OVERHEAT_OFF_MC` 50000, `RUPTURE_HP` 3600. Emitter State: `FAULT_GLOW_REACH_PX` 72, `MAX_FAULT_GLOW` (placeholder 10).

**Knob invariants (build lint and acceptance tests):** (1) `GROW_PX_PER_STEP` monotone in `sev`, sev 1 >= 1, sev 4 in (12, 18]; (2) `GROW_PX_PER_STEP[3]` x 10 <= `DRIP_FALL_PX_PER_FRAME` x `TRAIL_ANIM_FPS`; (3) `FROST_REACH_PX` monotone and `frost_reach(-2000 mC)` >= 336; (4) `DRIED_AFTER_STEPS` >= 7500; (5) the rolling-window flash count `F_win` = ceil((floor(`TRAIL_ANIM_FPS` / `ARC_HOLD_FRAMES`) + 1) / 2) <= `FLASH_MAX_PER_S` and `ARC_HOLD_FRAMES` >= 3 (a pair that violates it is rejected, not clamped: D.8b; `ARC_FLICKER_PER_S` is derived and read-only); (6) every `TRAIL_GLOW_RADIUS_PX` <= 72; (7) `EMISSIVE_OFF_DULL_PM` <= 1000; (8) no knob of this table changes with sanity, Perception Filter stage, hallucination class or player count (FT-2); (9) `SEV_GLOW_PM` monotone non-decreasing with `[0]` = 0, every `GLOW_BASE_PM` in 0-1000 and every `sev_min` in 1-4. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review:)* (10) for every arc count n in 1-3 and every emissive size, `sum_i(A_emis_i x 0.577 x swing_i_pm / 1000) / REGION_MIN_PX` < 0.09 (D.8b); (11) `DENSITY_BUCKETS_PER_LEVEL` in 2-3, so `density_level` 4 is reachable; (12) every `COVER16_FLOOR` is 0 or at least `DITHER_MIN_CELLS`; (13) `TRAIL_MAX_STAMPS` = 64 x ceil(`stamps_at_caps` / 64) = 704 and >= the `stamps_world` of `fx_mvp_worst` (661) and `fx_design_max` (618), so a legal MVP world does not log; (14) under the explicit integer form of D.5 the final (dried) stop begins at `mode_steps` = `DRIED_AFTER_STEPS` and never earlier, for every legal `DULL_STEPS` (4-8, odd widths included) and every legal `DRIED_AFTER_STEPS`. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings:)* (15) `WET_SRC_PX` is even and 6-12, `WET_MIN_PX` is 2, and the level count `L` is at least 3 (D.2d); (16) for every sampled `T`, `grow_steps` and `sev` of a coolant leak, `n_frost + n_wet` <= `ceil(trail_extent_px / 48)` and `wet_w` is monotone non-increasing in the piece offset (D.2d); (17) the live-shown stop `DULL_STEPS` - 2 is strictly below the dried stop for every legal `DULL_STEPS` (D.5).

## Visual/Audio Requirements

*The descriptor-to-visual table of record (all eighteen ids, with hue, sizes, band, what `intensity_pm` and the trail age (`grow_steps` and `mode_steps`) drive, STILL form, glow and owner of what is not drawn) and the five-behaviours-by-three-sizes table are in Detailed Design section 6 and are not duplicated here. This section adds the **event map**, the palette and contrast rules with their hexes, the motion strips, the glow table (data for Emitter State), the truth-channel classification, the asset list for `/asset-spec`, the retained captures, the audio statement and the engine notes. Art direction: the art-director was consulted and ratified or rewrote the rows noted below; where the art-director and the technical-artist disagreed the rulings decide (noted in line as "orchestrator ruling"). Hexes are the art bible's (`ART:370`-`ART:384`); L\* values are approximations from the art bible's luminance ladder (`ART:447`) and the hex beside them is the one to test.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

### V/A.1 Event map: what the player sees, hears and which channel carries it

*Audio classes are the Graph's `acoustic` ids (`LSG:155`-`LSG:166`) and are played by Adaptive Audio at the same anchor (FT-35); this system plays nothing. Every row is TRUTH (consequence channel) unless it says otherwise.*

| # | Event (cause) | VFX and sprite state | Audio class (not played here) | Band | Class | Rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| E1 | A fault begins (a descriptor appears: `F/` or `N/`, or field entry) | the source stamp's first `MIN_EXTENT_PX` 48 px at the fitting, seam, opening or rib; the sev 3+ accent; moving media start on the next frame | by `acoustic` (HISS, DRIP, SIZZLE ...) | F / W / D | TRUTH | FT-3, FT-8, T1 |
| E2 | Severity steps up or down (`N/`) | size swap s / m / l in one frame; the Graph resets the mode onset, so the extent restarts at the 48 px source stamp and regrows at the new rate (FT-8) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*; no tween, no flash | none new | as host | TRUTH | FT-6, T6 |
| E3 | Time passes (`grow_steps` grows; frozen while a valve isolates the leak) | the trail lengthens in 10 Hz steps by `GROW_PX_PER_STEP[sev]` (20 / 30 / 60 / 150 px/s); the front is ragged (frost) or radial (scorch) by the reveal threshold; growth tip frame from extent | none | F / W | TRUTH | FT-8, FT-23, D.2 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| E4 | A cascade hop (the Graph lights the next edge) | the next edge's source stamp appears at 48 px; this system holds no front position | the new descriptor's own class | F / W | TRUTH | FT-10 |
| E5 | A valve closes on a live coolant or fuel leak | the drip and the moving frames stop on the next frame; the fan, collar or streak **stays, still** and **stops growing at the isolation step**; dullness continues from `mode_steps` | DRIP stops (Audio) | F / W | TRUTH | FT-17, T3 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| E6 | A valve reopens | the drip and moving frames restart; stamps unchanged | DRIP resumes | D | TRUTH | T4 |
| E7 | A repair, cap, cut, salvage, breaker-and-clear or dormancy ends the descriptor; **a patch ends the LEAK descriptor and begins the PATCH_WEEP on the same step** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* | the trail is gone on the next frame: no fade, no sparkle, no completion cue (the reward is the absence); for a patch the fan, wet run and drip are gone and the small static row-27 weep is there on that same frame (a GAS patch leaves nothing drawn: the HISS carries it); a room-surface crust (row 20) stays while its band lasts | the class stops (after a patch the weep's own HISS, DRIP or TICK begins, Adaptive Audio) | F / W / D | TRUTH | FT-1, FT-32, T5, T11 |
| E8 | The room warms or cools | frost reach interpolates (D.3) so the fan retreats or advances continuously; frost stamps beyond the reach turn into coolant wet-run pieces tile by tile (they do not disappear: the run stays, D.2d), and the last piece is revealed through the threshold *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* | none | F / W | TRUTH | FT-11, D.3 |
| E9 | A `2 SHORT` arcs | radial scorch at the box with a Power Amber rim; spark tips pop (`vfx_pwr_arc_pop`), irregular dwell of at least 3 frames on and 3 off, at most `ARC_FLICKER_PER_S` flashes per second; the glow request is the glow-table row | SIZZLE, CLANK | W + F, pop in D | TRUTH | FT-12, FT-29 |
| E10 | A cable or box warms under overload (`7 OVERLOAD`) | scorch accumulates, a steady warm rim, no pops until row 2 takes over | HUM climb | W + F | TRUTH | row 7 |
| E11 | A machine overheats (`3 OVERHEAT`), then scrams (`9 SCRAM`) | sev 2: stepped shimmer at the fitting plus a small scorch at the port; sev 3-4: scorch in the loop hue; after SCRAM the rim's emissive layer wanes with the dullness ramp (`glow_step`), the scorch stays | none | W + F, shimmer in D | TRUTH | FT-24, row 3, row 9 |
| E12 | A hull plate or rib gives way (`5 BREACH`) | hairline (pre-stage, mode-5 STRUCT, confirmed) -> crack -> bent rib or hole along the rib or seam; debris and wind streaks toward the hole while there is flow; the hole is Void Steel only | HISS, WIND | W + F, debris in D | TRUTH | row 5, FT-31 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| E13 | A weld seam weeps (`1 LEAK/GAS` along a `SEAM` subject, sev 1, confirmed) | the mist along the raised Hull Grey bead, spreading along the seam line, with the legibility floor of 8 of 16 cells | HISS | Y-sorted at the host face, mist in D | TRUTH | FT-14 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| E14 | A hatch is under strain (`26 SEAL_STRAIN`) | a bowed rim (GAS) or rim frost (HEAT) sprite on the hatch frame; `dir` picks the side; the door sprite beneath may be corrupted, the strain sprite is not | CREAK, TICK | W (frame sprite) | TRUTH | row 26 |
| E15 | An opening carries a draught (`24 AIRFLOW`) | dust and debris streaks along `dir` (eight authored directions, never rotated), only while open | WIND | D | TRUTH | row 24 |
| E16 | A derelict section is instantiated, or a save is restored (`ALL`) | one frame reconciles the whole set: trails appear already grown and dulled to their `grow_steps` and `mode_steps`; no draw-in; dead faults STILL, mist absent | none | all | TRUTH | FT-16, T2, T10 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| E17 | Fuel pools or drains (`25 PUDDLE`) | the puddle's size tier and stamp count follow `fuel_puddle_ml` (D.2c); it shrinks only as the Graph's volume does | none (the body's "slows walking" rule) | F | TRUTH | row 25 |
| E18 | A room's band reaches FROST or SCORCH (`B/`, `20`, `21`) | crust patches on exposed hull-floor edges and lower faces (cold) or a soot wash at sev 4 (hot) | breath fog, shiver, burn (Suit) | F + W | TRUTH | rows 20, 21, D.2b |
| E19 | The player steps the zoom ladder | the same base-px set is drawn at the new scale; a zoom-out reveals every trail whose anchor enters the field; nothing is scaled, faded or added | none | all | TRUTH | FT-19, FT-34 |
| E20 | Reduced motion on | every moving strip holds frame 0 (its hold pose); extent, size, density, dullness continue | audio unaffected | D | TRUTH | FT-28 |
| E21 | Reduce flashing on | arc pops become the held glow at `ARC_HELD_GLOW_PM` (600 permille of peak, the one hold level); the scorch still draws | audio unaffected | glow | TRUTH | FT-29 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| E22 | The Perception Filter runs at any stage | **nothing changes**: trail pixels are bit-identical with the pass on or off; a pipe silhouette may bleed violet beside an unviolet fan | none | all | TRUTH | FT-2 |
| E23 | The room is dark | no non-emissive trail pixel is brighter than the hull-floor tint; the hiss, the cold and the draught still tell; a live emissive edge may be visible | the class plays | all | TRUTH | FT-26 |
| E24 | A pipe, machine, gauge or WORN wear is drawn | **not drawn here**: the module-body channel (sprite, gauge, inspect card) belongs to the module presenter and In-World Instruments, and may lie | by the machine | n/a | corruptible (not F&T) | `LSG:168`, FT-24 |

### V/A.2 Palette, semantics and what no trail may contain

| Trail word | Hex | L\* (approx.) | Role |
|---|---|---|---|
| Mist | O2 Cyan `#3FD6E8` | ~79 | O2 loop failure |
| Frost fan, crust, sheath; coolant wet run (2 px Ice edge, Void Steel body); coolant drip; room-surface cold | Coolant Ice `#C8E6F5` | ~91 | coolant loop failure, and the material of cold (shape, not loop, carries the meaning on a compartment descriptor) |
| Scorch rim, arc spark tips, SHORT and OVERLOAD glow | Power Amber `#F2C230` | ~80 | power loop failure; the rim takes the subject's loop hue for HEAT rows (FT-12) |
| Puddle edge, fuel drip, ember glint | Fuel Ember `#F06A28` | ~61 | fuel loop failure |
| Hairline lip, crack lip, bent-rib bead, seam bead tint | Hull Grey `#8C949C` | ~61 | integrity; thin elements only, never a plane (a 144 px integrity stamp is lip and bead geometry, the rib body is Graphite) |
| Scorch core, puddle body, hairline and crack shadow, frost under-shadow, hole | Void Steel `#0B1018` | ~5 | the dark partner of every trail |
| Soot wash, rib body, dust streak partner | Graphite `#2A303A` (mid) / `#5C6670` (lit) | ~20 / ~43 | neutral |

**Hue comes from the descriptor's `loop`, shape from its `medium`** (FT-12); no heat hue is invented.

**Forbidden in every trail sheet and every trail render (auto-scanned, AC-52 and AC-125):** Alarm Red `#E0102A` (appears in no loop and no trail); Chemlight `#86B896` (glowstick light only; an O2 mist lit by a Tungsten beam shifts toward teal near hue 160, Chemlight is hue 139 at about 27 percent saturation against the mist's about 67 percent, and they separate by saturation, by form and by motion: a free-standing pool around a source sprite versus a plume anchored to a fitting); Tungsten `#FFE2A8` and `#FFCC66` (human light only); **violet** (hue 270-330 at saturation above 25 percent) and **flesh** (hue 5-30 at saturation 20-60 percent and value 45-85 percent), which the sane world reserves for sanity leaks; web vocabulary (iridescence, hue-less branching rime, filament shapes: Horror's, `ART:565`); and any pixel that is not a palette hex or the unlit hull-floor tint in a sheet. F&T has no sanity input, so it emits neither reserved hue under any stage.

**The dulling ramp (D.5) and its palette catch.** A naive desaturation walks Fuel Ember (hue about 20, saturation above 80 percent, value about 94 percent) into the flesh band, and a dried stain would read as skin: a Pillar 1 violation produced by the honest channel. The per-hue endpoints are therefore fixed, at the default `DULL_STEPS` 4 (`dull_step` 0-3); the quantised steps keep captures bit-identical across GPUs and the scan matrix finite (orchestrator ruling CP-8: the technical-artist's and systems-designer's 4 is the placeholder, the art-director's 8 is the scan-matrix alternative):

| Hue | Fresh (`dull_step` 0) | Dried endpoint (`dull_pm` 1000, step 3) | Ramp rule |
|---|---|---|---|
| Fuel Ember | `#F06A28` | **Ember-dim**: hue 20 plus or minus 3, saturation at least 75 percent, value at most 38 percent (indicative `#5E2C12`; the constraint binds, the exact stop is the palette owner's) | a **palette swap** at the final stop only (`dull_step` = `DULL_STEPS` - 1, i.e. `mode_steps` >= `DRIED_AFTER_STEPS`) with no intermediate stop and no dither (D.5 `fuel_dried`); value descends first; never mix toward grey *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| Power Amber | `#F2C230` (hue about 45) | Hull Grey `#8C949C`, rim only | hue held at or above 38 degrees through the ramp (the art bible's sampling floor is 36); desaturate last |
| Coolant Ice | `#C8E6F5` | Hull Grey `#8C949C` (spokes are thin elements) | blue-grey throughout; safe |
| O2 Cyan | `#3FD6E8` | none: mist has no dried form (FT-18) | not ramped |
| Hull Grey | `#8C949C` | unchanged | integrity does not dull; its dried verdict is the hairline itself |

At 4 steps the fresh-hue share of the 16-cell dither is `HUE_CELLS` {16, 11, 5, 0} (D.5). Emissive layers switch off at `EMISSIVE_OFF_DULL_PM` 500. **Scan matrix (AC-41a and AC-41b):** the four ramped hues x `DULL_STEPS` x {Tungsten beam, Chemlight pool, ambient only} on engine screenshots.

### V/A.3 Luminance ladder and the dual-polarity contrast rule

Define, for a ground of luminance `g` in L\* (Graphite mid `#2A303A` is about 20, Graphite lit `#5C6670` about 43), `C(g) = max(Lmax_trail - g, g - Lmin_trail)` taken over the trail's rendered elements. **Required: `C(g)` >= 22 L\* at both g = 20 and g = 43, at every `dull_step`** (art bible checklist 13, `ART:1216`: a trail out-contrasts every floor it crosses). For dried stops the colour-pair contrast against Graphite lit must also be at least `TRAIL_MIN_CONTRAST_X100` 190 / 100 (1.90:1). The unlit floor (Void Steel tint, about 5) is expected to hide non-emissive trails (FT-26); only live emissive elements read there.

| Behaviour | Lmax / Lmin elements | C(43) | C(20) | Pass | Note |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Mist | 79 / 79 | 36 | 59 | yes | light-only: no dark partner needed |
| Frost fan, crust, sheath | 91 / 5 (under-shadow) | 48 | 71 | yes | Ice 91 against a Tungsten beam 92 is carried by a static fill versus a moving cone (`ART:477`), not re-solved here |
| Coolant wet run (user ruling D26) | 91 (2 px Ice edge) / 5 (Void Steel body; none at a 2 px width) | 48 | 71 | yes | the dark body is the dark partner and the 2 px edge the light one; dried at widths 8, 6 and 4: Hull Grey 61 edge / 5 body, C(43) 38 and C(20) 41; **the dried 2 px piece has no body, so a Hull Grey 61 edge alone gives C(43) 18, below the 22 L\* minimum (the failure the hairline was given a pair to cure): it is drawn as the hairline composite of FT-22, a 1 px Hull Grey run paired with a 1 px Void Steel shadow line, still 2 px wide, so the 8 / 6 / 4 / 2 width law of user ruling 6a and every count of D.2d are unchanged, and it gives C(43) 38 and C(20) 41** (AC-184); no new palette entry *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings; re-review)* |
| Scorch, fresh | 80 (Amber rim) / 5 (core) | 38 | 60 | yes | the dark core alone (5 against 20) fails; the rim fixes it |
| Scorch, dried | 61 (Hull Grey rim) / 5 | 38 | 41 | yes | |
| Puddle, fresh | 61 (Ember edge) / 5 (body) | 38 | 41 | yes | |
| Puddle, dried | edge Ember-dim about 30 / 5, plus Graphite-lit glint specks (43) | 38 | 23 | yes, only through the glint specks | without the specks C(20) = 15, a fail |
| Hairline, crack | 61 (Hull Grey) / 5 (shadow pair) | 38 | 41 | yes | Hull Grey 61 against Graphite lit 43 alone is 18, a fail; hence the pair |
| Bent rib, hole | 61 / 5 | 38 | 41 | yes | |
| AIRFLOW dust | 61 specks / 5 streak | 38 | 41 | yes | held to the same rule for legibility |
| Soot wash (`21` at sev 4), **with the Graphite-lit ash-fleck rim** | 43 (rim, `#5C6670`) / 5 (core) over a wash of 20 | 38 | 23 | yes | the light partner is a 2 px rim of ash flecks (Graphite lit, hue-less) along the wash edge; without it C(20) = 15 and the row failed by a declared exemption, which contradicted AC-74 on a sev-4 consequence-channel row *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |

**The five behaviours stay distinct in greyscale and in a still frame** (polarity x form, `ART:314`):

| | Polarity | Form predicate (the pass test of AC-26b and AC-74) | Forbidden look-alike |
|---|---|---|---|
| Mist | light | soft round dither clusters; no straight edge longer than 4 px; no 60 or 90 degree edge | mist has no angle |
| Frost | lightest | angular hex-stair spurs branching at 60 degrees; no round form | frost has no curve |
| Coolant wet run (a coolant form of the frost loop, not a sixth behaviour) | dark body, light edge | a long thin strip of parallel edges with a 2 px light edge on one long side, width stepping 8 / 6 / 4 / 2 px with distance from the fitting, no spur, no fork, no blob, no glint speck, no iridescence | a wet run has no crystalline spur (frost), no blob or glint (puddle), no fork (hairline) and no iridescence (Horror's smear, `ART:565`) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* |
| Scorch | dark | straight radial spokes of uneven length from one core | scorch has an origin; soot does not |
| Puddle | dark | one convex glossy blob, glint specks, no spokes | a puddle has no spokes |
| Hairline | mid plus dark pair | a thin run along a rib or seam, at most one fork per 16 px | a hairline runs along, never from a point |

### V/A.4 Sizes and sub-tiers

The s / m / l semantics (s = sev 1-2, m = sev 3, l = sev 4; s carries *trace* and *full* sub-tiers) and the five-by-three drawing table are FT-6 and Detailed Design section 6, ratified by the art-director. Size is a **pixel-level difference in the drawing, never a scaled copy** (FT-22). Floor cells are square (48 / 96 / 144); **wall cells are 48 / 96 / 144 wide and at most 72 tall** (the wall front face is 72 px, `ART:1033`), with ink at most 64 px tall (72 minus the 4 px margin of `ART` 8.6). The hole at `A_hole` >= 32 (`LSG:365`) has three authored variants (32-40, 41-52, 53-64 of `A_HOLE_MAX` 64) and shows Void Steel only: no web, no filament, no iridescence at a breach. **Reachable cells (FT-7):** a live `1 LEAK` and a forced `8 SEVERED` are always sev >= 3, so the MVP authors s-frost, s-scorch, s-hairline and s-strain **and s-mist (trace and full) and s-puddle** (the Graph states the seam weep at sev 1, and a small puddle is s by volume); `27 PATCH_WEEP` reuses these trace cells with no new art. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review; re-review)*

### V/A.5 Motion and animation constraints

| Element | Motion class | Frames | Rate | Constraint |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Static stamps (fan, crust, sheath, coolant wet run, scorch, puddle, hairline, crack, rib, bowed rim, rim frost, soot) | none; extent changes at the 10 Hz Graph step | 1 | n/a | extent is a pure function (D.2); optional smoothing only between two committed values, never extrapolation (`KERN:298`); no growth timer |
| Growth tip | stepped piece at the leading edge | 3 per behaviour | frame = `floor(extent / 48) mod 3` (a function of extent, not time) | visible only while extent is below its bounds; replaced by the terminal piece at the bound; no propagation animation state |
| Mist drift | stepped strip, loop | 6 | `TRAIL_ANIM_FPS` 12, phase from `present_time` | coverage constant within one dither cell between frames, so the region's mean luminance never swings 10 percent (PR-13) |
| Coolant and fuel drip | stepped strip | 8 (hang 2, fall 2, land 4) | 12 | **nothing in the cryo bay moves faster than the drip**: fall <= 6 px per frame (72 px/s) and the sev 1-3 fronts (20 / 30 / 60 px/s) are below it |
| Vent debris stream | stepped strip | 6 | 12 | chips Graphite lit, Hull Grey and Void Steel only |
| AIRFLOW dust | stepped strip | 4 per direction, 8 directions drawn, not rotated | 12 | speed is read from the frame step distance |
| Arc pop | stepped strip, including a hold frame | 6 | 12 | on-dwell at least `ARC_HOLD_FRAMES` (minimum 3) frames and off-dwell at least 3; at most `ARC_FLICKER_PER_S` flashes per second (derived, default 2); all arcs share one world phase *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* |
| Heat shimmer (sev 2) | stepped strip, rises 2 px per frame | 4 | 12 | a **stepped sprite at the fitting, not a displacement**; coverage at most 15 percent of cells, loop hue |
| Fuel glint | none | 1 | n/a | a static highlight from the specular map |

**Reduced-motion hold poses** (frame 0 of every strip; each must read as its behaviour word in a still image, FT-28): mist = the peak-coverage frame with one wisp leaning along `dir`; drip = a hanging bead with a 2 px streak below it; vent debris = a mid-flight cluster; AIRFLOW dust = directional streaks with a tapered tail; arc = a steady glow at 60 percent of peak with the four static Power Amber spark-tip pixels lit and no pops (the pose most first-run players see, so it must not read as a fixture lamp or an OVERLOAD rim, FT-29); shimmer = a static three-line wave. **Reading speeds as carried images:** cryo-bay frost is slow and thickest at the source (`ART:142`); a weld seam weep spreads along the bead while you watch (`ART:166`); a breach front is faster than a walk and no faster than a run, and the cascade front is one hop per Graph step (`ART:224`).

### V/A.6 Glow table (data for Emitter State; this system writes no light)

The emissive layer on scorch, arc and ember stamps is an **unshaded additive layer that casts no light** (FT-27). This table is **data** that Emitter State reads to derive a FAULT_GLOW emitter from the same descriptor (EM-17, `EM:200`; "a descriptor with no glow row creates no emitter"); Lighting renders it. The columns follow EM-17 and `EM:333`: `key` is the descriptor id plus medium, the index of `GLOW_BASE_PM[key]`; `radius_px` is in base px (Emitter State reads the sev 4 value, and the sev 3 value where sev 4 is n/a, `EM:543`) and a row with none inherits `FAULT_GLOW_REACH_PX` 72; the flicker class is bounded by FT-29; the light-request gate per row is this GDD's `sev_min[key]` column (`7` -> 3, `3` -> 3, others 1), read by Emitter State as its D.11 gate (`EM:572`); the exposure-ledger column carries this GDD's `GLOW_BASE_PM[key]` (permille at sev 4); the light-request colour carries 2D Lighting's per-loop `FAULT_*` hue token (LO-8, Ask E-A). Sizing anchors (`ART:1077`-`ART:1086`): fixture 144, deployable pool 120, glowstick pool 96, beacon 384; every trail light stays at most 72, so no trail light outranks a fixture (knob invariant 6).

| `key` | Descriptor | Overlay colour (the unshaded layer) | Light-request colour | `radius_px` (sev 3 / sev 4) | `sev_min` | Flicker class (overlay; light request) | Max rate | Reduce-flashing alternative | Exposure ledger (`GLOW_BASE_PM[key]`) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `2` | SHORT / ELECTRIC | Power Amber `#F2C230` rim and spark tips | Power Amber `#F2C230` (token `FAULT_ARC`, LO-8) | 48 / 72 | 1 | **pop** (irregular, never periodic; a visible spark source at the box); the light request pops on the same bounded class | `ARC_FLICKER_PER_S`, at most 3, default 2 | held glow at 60 percent of peak | `GLOW_BASE_PM` 800 [PLACEHOLDER] |
| `7` | OVERLOAD / ELECTRIC | Power Amber rim, steady | none at sev 1-2; Power Amber at sev 3 (token `FAULT_ARC`) | 24 / n/a (sev 3 is the ceiling for this mode in the MVP rows) | 3 | steady | none | same | `GLOW_BASE_PM` 600 [PLACEHOLDER] |
| `3` | OVERHEAT / HEAT | the subject's loop hue rim (FT-12) | the loop hue (the loop's token: `FAULT_ARC` power, `FAULT_O2` O2, `FAULT_EMBER` fuel; LO-8 has no `FAULT_*` token for a coolant- or integrity-loop subject: Open Questions 21) | 36 / 48 (no request at the sev 2 shimmer) | 3 | steady | none | same | `GLOW_BASE_PM` 500 [PLACEHOLDER] |
| `9` | SCRAM / HEAT | the loop hue rim, **wanes with `glow_step`** (D.5; the overlay only) | the loop hue (token as row `3`), at the emitter's `sev`-driven level | 36 / 36 | 1 (moot: a forced mode is sev >= 3) | steady; the **room light's `output_pm` follows `sev` only (EM-17)**, so the wane is the overlay's, not the emitter's | none | same | `GLOW_BASE_PM` 500 [PLACEHOLDER] |
| `1/FUEL` | LEAK / FUEL | Fuel Ember `#F06A28` edge | Fuel Ember `#F06A28` (token `FAULT_EMBER`) | 24 / 24 | 1 | steady glint (a static highlight, not a flicker) | none | same | `GLOW_BASE_PM` 600 [PLACEHOLDER] |
| `1/GAS` | LEAK / GAS (venting) | none (the mist is unlit) | O2 Cyan `#3FD6E8` (token `FAULT_O2`), **requested only while `intensity_pm` > 0** | 36 / 48 | 1 (plus the `intensity_pm` > 0 gate) | steady | none | same | `GLOW_BASE_PM` 400 [PLACEHOLDER] |
| none | `1/COOLANT`, `4`, `20`, `21`, `5`, `8` (per its medium row), `24`, `25`, `26`, `27` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: PATCH_WEEP requests no light)*, hull and strain rows | n/a | n/a | n/a | n/a | n/a | n/a | n/a | n/a: no glow row, no emitter |

Emitter State's D.11 (`EM:543`) computes `output_pm = floor(GLOW_BASE_PM[key] x SEV_GLOW_PM[sev] / 1000)` and names this GDD the owner of `SEV_GLOW_PM[0..4]` = {0, 250, 500, 750, 1000} [PLACEHOLDER: `SEV_GLOW_PM`] and co-owner of `GLOW_BASE_PM[key]` (`EM:759`-`EM:760`; the six values in the table above are those of its D.11 variable table, `EM:550`); this GDD accepts ownership of both arrays as glow-table row data; Emitter State owns the formula, the FAULT_GLOW record, `MAX_FAULT_GLOW` and `FAULT_GLOW_REACH_PX`.

*(Rulings on this table: (1) the cyan `1/GAS` row is written because EM-17 and `EM:83` name cyan O2 mist as a FAULT_GLOW source and "a descriptor with no glow row creates no emitter"; its request is gated on `intensity_pm` > 0 because a cyan light over an empty STILL plume would be a lie on the consequence channel, and Emitter State adopts the gate: its D.11 key row is "requested only while `intensity_pm` > 0" (`EM:549`) and the record is removed on the Graph tick `intensity_pm` ends (`EM:678`). (2) `25 PUDDLE` has no glow row: the Graph defines no `sev` for it, so there is no `SEV_GLOW_PM[sev]` index, and its ember edge is the static specular glint of the stamp only. (3) The art-director's `SCRAM_GLOW_DECAY_STEPS` is dropped: D.5's `glow_step` already governs the overlay's wane and EM-17 governs the emitter. (4) The knob `TRAIL_GLOW_RADIUS_PX[key]` is a pair (sev 3, sev 4); Emitter State reads the sev 4 value and the sev 3 value where sev 4 is n/a (`EM:543`), and a row with none inherits `FAULT_GLOW_REACH_PX` 72. (5) EM-17 as now worded (`EM:200`) names the six glow keys above; the earlier "descriptor ids 20..26" drift is closed.)*

**Light verbs** (fixed so a decoy, a flare and an arc cannot be confused in prose or captures): arcs *pop*; deployables *flicker*; the cryo lamp *gutters*; pipes *glow*; fuel edges *glint*. An arc pop is told from a decoy by (1) a visible spark source and a radial scorch on a fixed box, (2) Power Amber `#F2C230` at about 80 percent saturation against the Tungsten fixture `#FFCC66`, and (3) an irregular on and off dwell of at least 3 frames each, not a smooth wobble (the default held pose keeps its static spark tips, FT-29, which separates it from a lamp). **Luminance swing for the regional flash clamp (D.8b):** emissive area per cell at 40 percent emissive 922 / 3686 / 8294 px (s / m / l), `Y_peak` 0.577 (Power Amber), swing `swing_i_pm` = the lower of `ARC_SWING_PM` 240 and the D.8b bound (173 / 86 / 57 for one / two / three l arcs in a compartment), derived at the smallest region `REGION_MIN_PX` 9,216 px (the 25,560 px of the default 640 x 360 field is the largest-region case, not the worst), regional ceiling 0.09 (below the counted 0.10). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

### V/A.7 Truth-channel classification

**Every visual this system emits is TRUTH.** No element is corruptible; there is no `PERCEPTION_OUT` read and no per-player variant.

| Element | Class | Why |
|---|---|---|
| All stamps and strips of the table of record (fan, crust, sheath, mist, drip, scorch, puddle, hairline, crack, rib, hole, soot, dust, debris) | TRUTH | the closed consequence-channel list (`LSG:168`) |
| SEAL_STRAIN bowed rim and rim frost on the hatch frame | TRUTH | an OPENING descriptor on the closed list; a separate sprite attached to the frame, so the door sprite beneath may be corrupted while the strain mark stays |
| OVERHEAT and SCRAM shimmer at the fitting | TRUTH | modes 3 and 9; drawn at the fitting, never on the machine body |
| Emissive layers | TRUTH | part of their stamp |
| Glow-table light requests | not this system's | Emitter State and Lighting own them; truth is their rule |
| Pipe, machine, gauge, inspect card, WORN wear | corruptible (module-body), **not drawn here** | `LSG:168` |

Rules this section imposes on the tag contract (PR-10, `PIPE:206`, `PIPE:735`): (1) wall trails overlap pipe silhouettes (a frost fan is drawn over the lagging; the Filter's stage-3 bleed goes "at pipe silhouettes", `ART:232`, `ART:238`), so the truth mask must be **per trail element, not per layer**: a pipe edge may bleed violet beside a frost fan and the fan's pixels never do; (2) no phantom trail exists, so no hallucinated mist, frost or scorch sheet is authored; (3) a floor trail renders bit-identically with the Filter off and at its maximum stage (`PIPE:989`); (4) a false-fixed machine sprite beside a true leak leaves the stamp at the fitting unchanged.

### V/A.8 Layer, band and ordering

The bands are FT-25. **Authoring order inside the floor-marks band** (lowest first): puddle, frost crust, scorch, hairline and crack, then the emissive layers; liquids lie on the floor and crack lips must stay readable over soot and frost (V/A.3). The wall cap (z +20) draws over the top 24 px of a wall face: never ink on the cap. All bands stay inside every light range limit (`PIPE:139`).

### V/A.9 Art bible principles and sections applied

| Cite | Applies as |
|---|---|
| Principle 1 (`ART` 1) | trails are lit marks; a dark room hides them; the hull-floor tint (`ART:1248`); the acoustic and Suit channels tell in the dark |
| Principle 2 (`ART:44`-`ART:55`) | state lives in the sprite; the loop's own hue; no world-space icon layer; no per-state door tiles (hence the frame-attached strain sprite) |
| Principle 3 (`ART` 1) | emission is exposure: the glow table, decided by EM-17 |
| Corollary (`ART` 1) | the lie leaks violet at pipe edges; trails never do |
| 2.2, 2.4, 2.6, 2.8 | the cryo bay's "nothing moves faster than a drip"; weld-seam spread; dried trails; the retreat image |
| 3.3 | the five behaviours and the pipe-grammar table |
| 3.5 | hero shape 5: a trail out-contrasts the panel it crosses |
| 4.1, 4.2, 4.4, 4.5, 4.7 | palette, semantics, sanity palette, luminance ladder, colourblind rows (O2 versus coolant by drift versus static; Amber versus Ember by radial scorch versus glossy puddle; Hull Grey versus Ember by hairline versus puddle) |
| 5.5 | footprints are the web's vocabulary: this system never draws a branching or radial-from-contact mark |
| 6.3, 6.4, 6.5 | one trail per source; trail density per area type (cryo bay one trail, machine room "originates here", a dead breached section two to four dried, grafted home-ship rooms live in loop hue); the verdict grammar |
| 8.2, 8.3, 8.5, 8.6, 8.7, 8.9 | cell sizes, `_alb` / `_msk` / `_nrm` / `_spc`, naming, atlas cap, LOD gates, checklist items 4, 5, 8, 11, 13 |

Proposed loop tokens `drift`, `fall`, `stream`, `pop`, `rise`, `dust` (an amendment candidate to `ART:1133`-`ART:1135`, art-director owned). | `trail_pwr_scorch_<s,m,l>` (loop-hue rims by `_msk` R, not new sheets) | scorch | floor, wall | alb, msk | about 0.6 M |
| `trail_fuel_puddle_<s,m,l>` (s is reached by volume tier, D.2c) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | puddle | floor | alb, msk, nrm, spc | about 0.4 M |
| `trail_hull_hairline_s`, `trail_hull_crack_m`, `trail_hull_rib_l`, `trail_hull_seamweep_<s,m,l>`, `trail_hull_soot_<m,l>` | integrity | floor, wall | alb, msk | about 0.65 M |
| `trail_hull_rimbow_s`, `trail_cool_rimfrost_s` | strain | frame-attached, one per port shape | alb, msk | about 0.04 M per port shape (MVP: the round Foundry frame only) |

**Stepped media (`vfx` family, own page `vfx_trail_media`, 1024 squared, alb + msk, distinct from `vfx_shared` which holds Horror's footprints, the Tell and particle textures):** `vfx_o2_mist_drift_<s,m,l>` (6 frames; the s cell is the MVP seam weep's), `vfx_cool_drip_fall` and `vfx_fuel_drip_fall` (8 frames, 16 x 32), `vfx_hull_debris_stream` (6, 96), `vfx_airflow_dust_<dir>` (4 frames, 48, eight directions), `vfx_pwr_arc_pop` (6, 48), `vfx_heat_shimmer_rise` (4, 48 x 96); about 0.36 M px, one page at about 35 percent. Growth tips stay in the static family. **Not produced:** any phantom or hallucinated trail; any fresh-and-dried duplicate sheet (`ART:1066` rejects route (a)); any propagation-frame set (FT-21 drops it); any Alarm Red or Chemlight pixel. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

**Material and data assets.** `_msk` conventions: R = the loop-medium region (the pixels the dulling and emissive shader may touch); G = the flow-phase and reveal-order ramp, 7 bits (`RAMP_MAX` 127; D.10); B unclaimed by this system (the art-director's request to claim it for a coverage rank is the FT-23 mechanism ADR candidate, runtime rank discard versus pre-authored dither sets); A binary 0 or 255. The descriptor-to-glow table (V/A.6) and the table of record are shipped as data files. No palette-file additions (Ember-dim and the Amber and Ice dried stops are runtime ramp endpoints, not sheet pixels).

**Atlas scope (flagged to the producer, not decided here):** the enumerated static art is about 2.4 M px pre-cut (about 3.0 M with 25 percent atlas waste), which does **not** fit the 1024 squared page of `ART:1157` (about 1.05 M px) and fits a 2048 squared page at about 72 percent; with the MVP cut (Foundry only, variants s 2 / m 1 / l 1, transposes for floor runs and flips for corners) it falls to about 1.3 M px, still above 1024 squared. Recommendation: `trail_shared` alb and msk at 2048 squared, frost and puddle normal and specular on a separate 1024 squared page, media on its own 1024 squared page (about 25 MB of VRAM more than `ART:1169`, inside its "VRAM decides nothing" conclusion); this amends `ART:1157` and the merge advice of `ART` 8.6 (merge `seam_shared` only). The "5 behaviours x 3 sizes" estimate of 15 cells is a floor: chainable pieces, wall cells and the sub-tiers raise it 6-12 times (Open Questions: producer).

### V/A.11 Art-side acceptance captures (retained, lit, `production/qa/evidence/`; signing lead: art-director)

1. One capture per behaviour per size (5 x 3 = 15), the chest lamp's beam on the trail, at `k_auto` and the first zoom-out (AC-74).

8. Collision fixtures: frost fan against Horror's branching rime (Horror's rime must be hue-less, L\* 70-78, with non-repeating angles, never at a coolant fitting); O2 mist against a Chemlight pool; a RUPTURE trail against a footprint; the coolant wet run against Horror's wet iridescent smear (AC-79).

10. The derelict verdict set: fan, radial scorch, ember stain, hairlines and the absence of mist readable in one beam (AC-43). *(The radial scorch leg is conditional on the Graph emitting a dead electrical descriptor: Open Questions.)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
11. The seam weep still: mist along a seam at `A_seam` 1, `k_auto`, lit by the chest lamp (AC-151). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review.)*

15. The patch three-way contrast *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: the same coolant elbow with the valve closed (full still fan), patched (the small row-27 weep) and capped (nothing), and a patched duct (nothing drawn, HISS only), lit (AC-179).

### V/A.12 Audio

**None.** This system plays no sound and gates nothing on sound. The `acoustic` classes (HISS, DRIP, HUM_DROP, CLANK, WIND, TICK, SIZZLE, CREAK) are Adaptive Audio's, played at the same anchor and allowed to lead the visuals (`IDX:41`). A hiss in a dark room with no visible mist is expected, not a defect. Mood only: the sound at a trail should feel physical and local to the fitting, in a world where the floor does not lie.

### V/A.13 Engine notes (Godot 4.7.2; behaviour-level, every claim below is an ADR candidate)

Godot 4.7.2 is pinned (`docs/engine-reference/godot/VERSION.md`; the model's knowledge covers roughly 4.3, so every 4.5-4.7 claim is cross-checked there). The reference folder `docs/engine-reference/godot/modules/` holds animation, audio, input, navigation, networking, physics, rendering and ui; **there is no particles, canvas-item, shader or scripting/core file**, so every statement about particle nodes, canvas-item materials, the `_msk` shader and batching below is `[UNV]`, to be verified by the spikes. Items taken from `breaking-changes.md`: the 4.7 canvas line drawing adds no antialiasing feather (moot here: lines are sprites, FT-22); the particle `process_time` rename and `process_time_residual` addition on the four particle nodes; the optional `keep_seed` parameter on particle `restart()`; Shader Baker pre-compilation (4.5). `DrawableTexture2D` is not used in the MVP.

Spike list (owned by the technical-artist; none blocks this GDD; **S2 is a gate on story start**, see below): **S1** particle path, only if venting leaves stepped frames: per-instance positions floor to the base grid and alpha stays binary under nearest sampling (zero sub-pixel positions and zero intermediate alpha in a 60 s capture at every k and at both viewport passes); **S2 (render structure and per-instance data route; a gate on the first render story)** floor-marks batching on D3D12 with one atlas page and the shared mark material, per-instance uniform cost, wall-row batching, the 2D light-set term `L_sets` and the Y-sort interleave term `I_wall` (`D_FT` <= 38 at k = 1 on 2160p with 704 stamps and 16 wall rows). S2 must choose the render structure (a per-trail `_draw`, a `MultiMesh` with custom data, or a `Sprite2D` pool) and prove the route by which the per-instance reveal threshold, dull step, coverage rank and clock reach the shader in 4.7.2 (`[UNV]`: the engine reference has no canvas-item file); S2 reports three separate pass lines *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: (a) whether `CanvasItem` shaders receive per-instance data at all, (b) whether the four values survive exact 8-bit packing under `hdr_2d` (every one of the 256 levels of each channel reads back equal to the value written), and (c) 64 distinct parameter sets drawn in one batch without a draw split; S2 also decides whether the animation frame is shader-driven from a shared `present_time` uniform (D.7); the **fallback** if per-instance custom data is not available to `CanvasItem` shaders is to pack those four values into the instance `COLOR` channels and the `_msk` G ramp, at the cost of a coarser dull step; if neither route works, batching collapses toward one draw per stamp and `D_FT_MAX` is re-derived at the ADR. **The S2 result is recorded in `docs/engine-reference/godot/` (a new canvas-item file) and in an ADR before the first render story starts** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*; **S3** stamp chaining at 48 / 96 / 144 and wall cells against 48 px tiles, the 24 px floor depth and the 72 px face, with a 2 px atlas gutter (no seam, no overhang into the cap band, no opaque pixel outside the compartment); **S4** material warm-up and Shader Baker (the first leak's frame time <= 1.5 x the render median); **S5** the arc flash scan with glow on (two l arcs in one region, and two l arcs in adjacent compartments: `F` <= 3 in every 1 s window per light, region and frame mean) and the timing of an `ALL` reconcile at `fx_mvp_worst` (661 stamps) and `fx_design_max` (618), pass thresholds in D.7; **S6** the truth-channel mask under both Filter routes (Filter-owned); **S7** lit Coolant Ice against the glow threshold at beam centre (no bloom on any lit trail sprite at any light energy Lighting allows: Coolant Ice's blue channel passes 1.0 at a beam-centre energy of about 1.10 under `hdr_2d`, so the glow threshold must sit above the brightest lit trail albedo and below the emissive layers' peak; Lighting decides the mechanism).

ADR candidates raised: the split between the notification handler and the render callback (PR-8 rule 3); stepped frames versus particles for moving media; per-instance reveal-threshold and coverage-rank plumbing that keeps alpha binary; the shared "hull surface mark" material accounting (the art bible's five-material budget, `ART:1241`, must either already count the pipe-flow fill, the hull-floor term and the additive layer as one shared material or this system adds one: Open Questions); `present_time` delivered to the shader as a shared value instead of the engine clock (the engine clock does not freeze with the kernel PAUSED); a pure `RefCounted` resolver from descriptor to trail specification so the decision logic is headless-testable; the z-index numbering inside the floor-marks band; the truth-channel mask route (Filter-owned).

> 📌 **Asset Spec** — Visual/Audio requirements are defined. After the art bible is approved, run `/asset-spec system:failure-and-trail-system` to produce per-asset visual descriptions, dimensions, and generation prompts from this section.

## Game Feel

*This system takes no input and owns no action; its "feel" is how fast and how heavily the ship's handwriting appears, grows, stills and goes, measured from the moment the Graph changes. Everything below is a number or a pass/fail observation; "feels like dread" is not a specification, and each target traces to a rule, a formula or a registry constant. The weight of the system is geological, not kinetic: nothing here snaps, shakes, freezes the game or punctuates with a flourish. The punctuation is the absence of an effect (a cap or a cut leaves nothing; a patch leaves a small weep, never a flourish) and the one-frame size jump.*

### Feel Reference

**Moment borrowed:** the hull-breach and flooding reads of *Barotrauma*, where damage is drawn where it is, on the wall you then walk to with a tool, and nothing in a screen corner tells you where; and the hiss-in-the-dark of *Alien: Isolation*, where a leak is a sound first and a thing you must spend light to see. Taken precisely: the player finds the fault by following the mark's gradient to its thickest point (FT-3), not by a locator. **Anti-reference:** *Dead Space* (2008)'s floor locator line and the *Arkham* / *Witcher* detective vision, which turn reading into a button press (Player Fantasy, reference techniques); and any trail that animates in with a flourish, which would make the Graph look like it is performing for the player.

### Input Responsiveness

*The chain from a player's verb to a trail's change is: verb -> Graph mutator -> the next Graph run (hooks at divisor `GRAPH_STEP_TICKS` 6 ticks = 100 ms at `TICK_RATE` 60) -> the dirty key (delivered once per tick in P5) -> this system's next render callback. This system adds at most one render frame to the Graph's own latency; the Graph's latency is the Graph's (LS-8). Frames below are at 60 frames per second (16.7 ms).*

| Action or event | Max latency from the Graph's state change to the visible change | Frame budget at 60 fps | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| A valve closes (V5) and the Graph reports `intensity_pm` 0 | next render frame after the key: drip and moving frames stop | at most 1 frame after the key; at most 7 frames (about 117 ms) after the verb completes, worst case a full Graph step plus one frame | the fan stays, still; the extent stops growing; dullness does not jump (AC-36, AC-146) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| A cap, cut or salvage ends the descriptor, or a patch hands the LEAK to the PATCH_WEEP (T5, T11) | next render frame after the key; no fade | 1 frame (about 117 ms from the verb, worst case) | no alpha below 255 at any frame, and no frame shows both the old trail and the weep (AC-114, AC-70) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* |
| A fault begins (a descriptor appears) | the frame after the key: the first `MIN_EXTENT_PX` 48 px of the source stamp | 1 frame after the key | the Graph's own tick delivered the descriptor; moving media start on the same frame |
| A severity step (`N/`) | next render frame: the new size and the restarted extent (`MIN_EXTENT_PX`) together *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | 1 frame | no tween, no intermediate frame (AC-19, AC-147) |
| A band or temperature change (`B/`, `C/`) | frost reach re-read on the next per-step poll; the retreat is continuous | at most 1 Graph step (100 ms) | no frame shows the new size with the old reach (AC-117) |
| Zoom ladder step | same frame as the camera field change: the in-field set is re-evaluated from the poll cache | 1 frame | no key, no state (FT-19, AC-46) |
| Reduced motion or reduce flashing toggled | next render frame | 1 frame | extent, size, density and dullness continue |
| The chest lamp's beam reaches a trail | the same frame Lighting lights the pixel | 0 additional frames | this system draws albedo; light is Lighting's |
| `ALL` (restore, derelict instantiation, cascade above `MAX_DIRTY_KEYS` 256) | one render frame; no draw-in | 1 frame; `TRAIL_RECONCILE_BUDGET_MS` (4 ms, in play) and `TRAIL_RESTORE_BUDGET_MS` (8 ms, restore) are logging thresholds and never delay the frame | AC-31 |

### Animation Feel Targets

*"Startup" is the delay from the Graph's key to the first drawn pixel; "Active" is the loop while the descriptor lives; "Recovery" is the delay from the descriptor's end to the last pixel. Stepped strips run at `TRAIL_ANIM_FPS` 12 (83.3 ms per frame), phase from `present_time`.*

| Element | Startup | Active | Recovery | Feel goal | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Static stamps (fan, scorch, puddle, hairline, crack, rib) | 0 (drawn whole on the frame of the key) | indefinite; extent grows at the Graph's 10 Hz in steps of `GROW_PX_PER_STEP[sev]` px | 0 (gone the next frame) | heavy and slow: a mark that does not perform | the growth front is ragged (frost) or radial (scorch) from the reveal threshold; no scale, no tween |
| Growth tip | 0 | 3 frames chosen by extent (`floor(extent / 48) mod 3`), not by time | 0 | a front that is a place, not a clock | D.8 |
| Mist drift | 0 | 6-frame loop = 500 ms | 0 | soft drift, no angle | reduced motion holds frame 0 |
| Drip | 0 | 8-frame cycle = 667 ms (hang 2 frames = 167 ms, fall 2 frames = 167 ms, land 4 frames = 333 ms); cadence `DRIP_PER_S` 1 / 2 / 4 / 6 by phased overlapping instances | 0 | the fastest motion in the cryo bay: 6 px per frame = 72 px/s | `ART:142` |
| Vent debris stream | 0 | 6-frame loop = 500 ms; `FRAMES_ACTIVE` 2 / 4 / 6 / 6 by density level | 0 | a tearing stream toward the hole | |
| AIRFLOW dust | 0 | 4-frame loop = 333 ms per direction | 0 | speed read from the frame step | |
| Arc pop | 0 | 6-frame strip; each on or off state held `ARC_HOLD_FRAMES` 3 frames = 250 ms (the minimum is 3); `ARC_FLICKER_PER_S` 2 flashes per second (derived), never above `FLASH_MAX_PER_S` 3 | 0 | irregular, never periodic: a fault, not a strobe. **The default look (reduce flashing ON, user ruling D79) is the held glow with its four static spark tips, not pops: a lit, tipped, radial fault** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* | AC-64, AC-63c |
| Heat shimmer | 0 | 4-frame loop = 333 ms, rising 2 px per frame = 24 px/s | 0 | a held breath over the fitting | a sprite, not a displacement |
| Dullness (`dull_step`) | n/a | one palette step per `DRIED_AFTER_STEPS` / (`DULL_STEPS` - 1) = 4000 Graph steps = 6.7 minutes at the placeholders, the dried stop at 12000 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | n/a | the ship ages by minutes, never by seconds | D.5 |
| Frost retreat with warmth | n/a | continuous in 10 Hz steps; at the cryo-bay heater's 8 mC per step the *frost part* (`frost_px`) falls from 336 to 192 px between 62.5 s and 100 s after power-on while the extent stays 336 and the rest of the chain turns to wet run (D.2 example, D.2d) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: it said the extent falls)* | n/a | the room visibly warming | AC-25 |

### Impact Moments

*There is no hit-stop, no screen shake, no controller rumble and no time-scale change in this system, by design (a trail is a consequence, not an event; camera tremor on a breach, if any, belongs to the Pipeline and is never suppressed by reduced motion, `PIPE:847`). The punctuation is below.*

| Impact type | Duration | Effect description | Configurable? |
|---|---|---|---|
| First appearance of a fault | 0 ms (one frame) | the source stamp's first 48 px at the fitting, with the sev 3+ accent; the drip's first hang frame; no flourish | no |
| Severity step to sev 3 or sev 4 | 0 ms (one frame) | the stamp swaps to the m or l drawing and the extent restarts at the 48 px source ("it just got serious": a new, heavier mark begins at the source); this is the system's one intended flow break *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | no |
| The breach front (sev 4) | 2.0 s to cross 288 px at 15 px per step (150 px/s), between the 120 px/s walk and the 180 px/s run | a hairline jumps to a bent rib that begins at the source, and the mark's age front (its extent, not a hazard boundary) races along that one edge; behind it the cascade front lights the next edge one hop per Graph step, which is the Graph's propagation and not a race *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* | `GROW_PX_PER_STEP[4]` in 13-18 only |
| An arc pops (the opt-out look; the default is the held glow with its spark tips) | 250 ms per state (3 frames, the minimum and the default), at most 2 per second | the rim and spark tips flash, then hold dark | `ARC_HOLD_FRAMES` 3-6; `ARC_FLICKER_PER_S` derived, at most 3 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* |
| A repair, cap or cut lands (a patch: the fan goes and a small weep stays) | 0 ms: the trail is gone on the next frame | the reward is the absence: no sparkle, no completion cue, no fade; after a patch the small static weep is the patch's own truth, not a reward | no (a fade would be presentation state, FT-1) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* |
| A valve closes | 0 ms for the drip; the fan stays | "still, not gone": the player's act is visible as a stop, not as an erasure | no |

### Weight and Responsiveness Profile

- **Weight:** heavy, slow and deliberate. The marks have the weight of residue: 20 / 30 / 60 / 150 px per second at sev 1 / 2 / 3 / 4 (`GROW_PX_PER_STEP` placeholders 2 / 3 / 6 / 15), a six-tile room (288 px) crossed in 14.4 / 9.6 / 4.8 / 2.0 s. The sev 4 front is the one fast thing, and it is fast on purpose: 150 px/s is strictly above the 120 px/s walk (`V_WALK` 2 px per tick) and no faster than the 180 px/s run (`V_RUN` 3 px per tick), so on that one edge a walking player is overtaken by the front and a running player is not (the front is the mark's age, not a hazard boundary; the cascade across edges is the Graph's and is not a race).
- **Player control:** the player has none over a trail and does not need any: every control belongs to the Graph (valve, patch, breaker, cut, warmth). The only feedback loop the player owns is *finding*: where the lamp points and how far they walk. Zooming out reveals more of the same marks at the same base-px size; it never helps by adding a layer.
- **Snap quality:** binary. Pixel art with alpha 0 or 255, nearest sampling, integer positions, no scale animation, no partial alpha (FT-22). Time is quantised: extent steps at 10 Hz, frames at 12 fps, sev changes in one frame.
- **Acceleration model:** none. Growth is a constant rate per severity, with no ease-in and no ease-out (a smooth curve would need presentation state, FT-1); the only non-constant behaviours are the one-frame size swap and extent restart when `sev` changes, the freeze of growth when a valve isolates the leak, and the continuous retreat of frost with temperature. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **Failure texture:** fair, with a read on why. Before any endogenous loss the Graph guarantees a sev >= 1 descriptor for at least `W_MIN_WARNING_S` 10 s (100 steps) and this system makes it seen, with a far-elbow trail crossing a six-tile room inside the 10-30 s tell window (28.8 s at the range floor of 1 px per step; 14.4 s at the placeholder 2 px per step); when a loop fails anyway the player can name the cause because the mark ran from the failing fitting. The trails are readable inside the Graph's own dread clocks (D.15's R21 targets: power about 4.5 minutes, coolant about 9, a sealed O2 room about 6, a breach event 90-150 seconds): even the shortest, the breach, leaves at least the 10 s window and a front the player can outrun.
- **Eye line:** the composition leads the eye trail -> source -> hatch (`ART:704`): the mark is thickest at the fitting, the fitting is at the end of the gradient, and the retreat route is the hatch behind the player.

### Feel Acceptance Criteria

*Measurable ones are automated (AC ids in brackets); the rest are playtest observations with a stated target that is a [PLACEHOLDER] until the first playtest.* **Playtest protocol PT-1** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the targets had no protocol)*: five first-time participants who have not seen the build or the art bible; the fixtures `FX-CRYO` (target 1) and `fx_mvp_worst` rooms with a lit sev 3-4 trail (targets 2-3); the default 640 x 360 field at 1080p, sound on, no prompts, no hints; the moderator says only "play"; time is read from the build's frame log (the frame of the first beam contact and the frame the participant stands within 48 px of the source), not from a stopwatch; "describes" is the participant's own words in a post-session free-text question, coded by two raters against the banned-word list of the Player Fantasy (marker, waypoint, highlight, indicator); the target is met when at least 4 of 5 participants pass, and a miss logs the fixture and the participant, not a defect.

- [ ] The sev 4 front moves 150 px/s: faster than the 120 px/s walk, not faster than the 180 px/s run, in a fixture with a walking and a running player (AC-17).
- [ ] In the cryo-bay fixture the sev 3 front is 60 px/s and the drip 72 px/s, so the drip is the fastest moving element (AC-18, AC-24b).
- [ ] A 288 px room is crossed in at most 28.8 s at every severity (AC-16).
- [ ] Every visible change lands on the render callback after its key and never before it (AC-142).
- [ ] A valve closing stops the drip within one render frame of the key, the fan does not move, vanish or jump in colour on the toggle, and the extent stops growing (AC-36, AC-146). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- [ ] A patch replaces the LEAK trail by the small static weep (or by nothing for a GAS subject) within one render frame of the key, with no frame below alpha 255 and no frame showing both (AC-114, AC-70, AC-179). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*
- [ ] No more than 3 flashes per second per light, per region and on the frame mean, with two l arcs in one region (AC-64).
- [ ] Every moving strip's frame 0 is identifiable as its behaviour word in a still frame (AC-63b, AC-63c).
- [ ] The first leak of a session draws within 1.5 x the median frame time (AC-84, spike S4).
- [ ] Playtest target [PLACEHOLDER]: at least 4 of 5 first-time playtesters in the cryo bay walk to the lagged elbow within 60 s of waking without a prompt, and describe the frost as "coming from" it (the gradient teaches the source).
- [ ] Playtest target [PLACEHOLDER]: no playtester describes any trail as a "marker", "waypoint", "highlight" or "indicator", and none asks where a sound came from after seeing a lit trail at its anchor (the mark and the hiss agree).
- [ ] Playtest target [PLACEHOLDER]: at k = `k_auto` and the first zoom-out a lit sev 3 or 4 trail is spotted in under 5 s of the beam reaching it by at least 4 of 5 playtesters; at k = 1 only form class and polarity are expected, and a missed sev 1-2 hairline is logged, not a defect (FT-34).
- [ ] While a sev 1-2 hull hairline is unresolved at k = 1, the hull-fatigue pre-stage still has a perceivable consequence-channel cue: the TICK acoustic class is played at its anchor by Adaptive Audio (AC-144, now group J since the Graph assigns TICK to the flow-zero pre-stage; Adaptive Audio's side is AC-AA-117). No marker or layer substitutes for it (FT-34). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

## UI Requirements

**No UI of its own; surfaces owned by the Graph's three diegetic surfaces and In-World Instruments.** This system adds no HUD element, no menu, no screen, no tooltip, no map, no schematic, no icon layer, no numeric readout and no alarm (FT-4; `LSG:1003`; `ART:44`-`ART:55`). A trail is a mark in the world drawn by the world's own rules, and it is read by spending light and walking to it. Three kinds of surface touch this system without being owned by it:

| Surface | Owner | What this system gives or takes |
|---|---|---|
| The Graph's three diegetic surfaces (the instruments, the lamp and the world marks of `LSG:1003`) | Life-Support Graph, In-World Instruments & Inspect View (provisional) | the trail is the physical witness each instrument row is checked against (LS-22, `LSG:205`); the pair shares no node (AC-138); nothing here is parented to a gauge, an inspect card or a machine body |
| Body cues (breath, vapour, visor fog, rime, shiver; Suit's SE-27 set, which has no vignette and no ear-pressure screen effect) | Suit & Equipment / HUD | none: `ATMO_THIN` and `ATMO_DECOMP` draw nothing here; the draught is `24 AIRFLOW` |
| The two accessibility toggles (reduced motion, reduce flashing) | Settings / Accessibility (provisional) | this system reads them (FT-36) and owns neither |

**What the two toggles do to this system, and nothing else:** *reduced motion* holds every moving medium on its authored hold pose (V/A.5), keeping direction, density, size, extent and dullness, and never suppresses a trail, a hairline or audio (FT-28, `PIPE:847`); *reduce flashing* replaces arc pops with a held glow at `ARC_HELD_GLOW_PM` (600 permille of peak) (FT-29, D.8b). Neither toggle can hide a trail. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

**Accessibility properties this system guarantees by form, not by setting** (the trail is information, so it must not depend on hue or motion alone): the five behaviours stay distinguishable in greyscale and in a still frame by polarity and form (V/A.3, AC-74, AC-76); O2 and coolant are told apart by "drifting mist versus static crystalline frost" with hue as the second cue (`ART:476`); Amber and Ember scorch and puddle by "radial scorch versus glossy puddle"; Hull Grey and Ember by "hairline versus puddle"; every hold pose reads as its behaviour word in a still image (AC-63c). No trail has a text label, so there is nothing to localise; the table of record's names are developer words, not player-facing strings. Legibility at the ladder floor is a stated gate, not a UI feature: full at `k_auto` and the first zoom-out, form class and polarity at k = 1 (FT-34); the remedy is in the stamp, never in an added layer.

**UX flag, routed and not raised by this system:** the Graph's `LSG:1003` UX flag (the three diegetic surfaces need `/ux-design`, and trail legibility is an input to In-World Instruments) belongs to the Graph and to In-World Instruments; this system supplies the inputs only: the greyscale forms of V/A.3, the hold poses of V/A.5, the `k` legibility gate of FT-34 and the no-added-layer rule. A future pattern in `design/ux/interaction-patterns.md` for "the world mark" would cite this GDD's V/A.3 and the Graph's closed consequence-channel list rather than restate them.
