### Registry hand-off

Written from the final text into the stage scratchpad's `registry-candidates.yaml` for the registry stage (this GDD writes no registry entry). **New formulas:** `trail_extent` (D.2), `frost_reach` (D.3), `trail_size` (D.1), `dullness` (D.5), `arc_flash_share` (D.8b), `trail_budget_at_k` (D.9), `trail_reconcile_cost` (D.7, registered 2026-10-27, paired with `dirty_key_budget`). **New constants:** `GROW_PX_PER_STEP`, `TRAIL_EDGE_MAX_PX`, `FROST_REACH_PX`, `DRIED_AFTER_STEPS`, `TRAIL_GLOW_RADIUS_PX`, plus `GLOW_BASE_PM[key]`, `SEV_GLOW_PM[0..4]` and `sev_min[key]` (V/A.6). **`referenced_by` additions:** `dirty_key_budget` (and the `REG:449` comment moves into that entry), `fairness_gate`, `rng_derive`, `flash_count`, `state_hash`, `world_scale_k`, `zoom_out_k`, and the 24 constants listed under "Cited, not tuned here". **Deliberately not registered (consumer-side, read by no other GDD; a deviation from R23's list, logged):** `trail_density`, `trail_stamp_variant`, `anim_frame`, `trail_reveal`, `surface_patch_count`, `puddle_stamps` and the internal knobs of Tuning Knobs; the registry stage may add any of them from D.4, D.6 without a GDD change.

**Design-review changes owed to the registry (2026-10-27; this GDD edits no registry entry):** `trail_extent` (the age is `grow_steps`, the Graph's GA-T field; the expression drops `sev_g` and `20 ATMO_COLD` from the cold rows; a severity change restarts the age; isolation freezes it), `dullness` (the age is `mode_steps`; `dull_step = DULL_STEPS - 1 if mode_steps >= DRIED_AFTER_STEPS else floor(mode_steps x (DULL_STEPS - 1) / DRIED_AFTER_STEPS)`; `fuel_dried` at the final stop only), `trail_size` (the seam sev-0 exception removed), `arc_flash_share` (one shared-phase rate, amplitude `swing_i_pm` derived at `REGION_MIN_PX` 9216, one hold level `ARC_HELD_GLOW_PM` 600), `trail_budget_at_k` (the `L_sets` and `I_wall` terms, a raised `TRAIL_MAX_STAMPS` and a recomputed MVP worst case; both figures are superseded by the re-review paragraph below), `trail_reconcile_cost` (the output range at the three fixtures). The ownership split is unchanged. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review.)*

**Owed to the registry after the 2026-10-27 user rulings (this GDD edits no registry entry)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*: `trail_extent` (the coolant leak is no longer a temperature-bounded row: `reach_px` is `edge_len_px` except for `4 FREEZE`); `frost_reach` (its role for `1 LEAK/COOLANT` is the bound of `frost_px`, an appearance split, not an extent bound; values unchanged); `dullness` (adds `dull_step_shown = min(dull_step, DULL_STEPS - 2)` while LIVE; `fuel_dried` reads `dull_step_shown`); `arc_flash_share` (the rate is `ceil(floor(TRAIL_ANIM_FPS / ARC_HOLD_FRAMES) / 2)`, the ruled flash unit `ceil(n_transitions / 2)`; defaults unchanged at 2); a new formula `coolant_wet_run` (D.2d) and the internal constants `WET_SRC_PX` 8, `WET_MIN_PX` 2 and `WET_PITCH_PX` 48; `trail_budget_at_k` has no number change (the wet run replaces frost slots one for one).

**Owed to the registry after the 2026-10-27 re-review (this GDD edits no registry entry)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: `trail_extent` (the age is the Graph's adopted GA-T `grow_steps` with no `since_steps` fallback, and the reader comment seeds `mode_since_step` with its matching `mode_key`, not `fail_since_step`); `dullness` (the same age change; `dull_step_shown` caps while `cap_applies`, which adds the ongoing static rows 7 and 27); `arc_flash_share` (`ARC_HOLD_FRAMES` range 3-6, the rolling-window lint `F_win`, `ARC_FLICKER_PER_S` derived and read-only, `n_arcs_room` counted over the 272 x 216 base-px neighbourhood); `trail_budget_at_k` (`TRAIL_MAX_STAMPS` 704 derived from the class caps, `M_wall` data-driven, `D_FT_MAX` conditional on spike S2, the MVP worst case 661 stamps and 63 descriptors); `trail_reconcile_cost` (the poll term in the cascade figure, four frame classes, `TRAIL_STEP_FRAME_MS_P99` 1.5, the new `TRAIL_PAN_FRAME_MS_P99` and `TRAIL_ANIM_FRAME_MS_P99`, the in-play `ALL` diff with the two thresholds `TRAIL_RECONCILE_BUDGET_MS` 4 and `TRAIL_RESTORE_BUDGET_MS` 8, the unit costs `C_FIELD_US` and `C_FRAME_US`); `coolant_wet_run` (a note: the dried 2 px piece is the hairline composite; width law unchanged).

## Acceptance Criteria

*Format: GIVEN / WHEN / THEN, one or more per core rule and per formula, then cross-system and budget criteria. Tags: **(auto)** an automated unit or integration test on the pure resolver (FT-39) or the poll logic that runs **headless** in the mandated CI command (`tests/unit/failure_and_trail/`, `tests/integration/failure_and_trail/`), BLOCKING; **(auto-render)** an automated test that needs rendered pixels (captures, bit-identity, dither, containment) and runs on the named render job `ft-render` (FT-40), never on the headless job, BLOCKING; **(lint)** a static or build-time scan, BLOCKING; **(manual)** a retained capture in `production/qa/evidence/` with sign-off by the named lead, BLOCKING (Visual/Feel and UI evidence is not waived at `qa.level: minimal`); **(spike)** needs a measured spike, named by id, with its pass threshold stated, before the story can close. Fixtures are the data files of `tests/fixtures/failure_and_trail/` (FT-40: `FX-CRYO`, `FX-SEAM`, `FX-TELL`, `fx_row_oracle.json` and the three budget fixtures). Performance criteria name the reference machine `REFERENCE_MACHINE` **[PLACEHOLDER: `REFERENCE_MACHINE`]** (`project.yaml` names none; the producer names it before the first spike closes). **Measurement protocol for every frame-time criterion** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: the criterion states its sample (at least 600 Graph steps, about 3,600 render frames, of a scripted player walking at `V_WALK`, and at least 100 samples in every frame class it judges), its percentile method (nearest-rank p99 over the samples of that class) and writes a **host header** into its evidence file (CPU, GPU, OS, driver, display mode and whether the run was an editor or an exported run, as recorded by the harness). The header records what ran; it names no required machine or build, and X14 stays open. **Reviewer pool RP-3 (defined once, cited by every reviewing criterion):** three reviewers, none of whom authored the capture and none of whom has seen its label: the art-director, the qa-lead and one participant from outside the team (a PT-1 participant); each classifies independently and in writing before any discussion; unless a criterion states another threshold, a classification passes when all three are correct for m and l at `k_auto` and the first zoom-out, and at least two of three are correct for s-tier captures and for k = 1. Citation form `[FT-n; LS-n; PR-n; CR-n; SL-n; EM-n; pillar]`: `LS-` the Life-Support Graph, `PR-` the Pipeline, `CR-` the kernel, `SL-` Save/Load, `EM-` Emitter State; `D.n` a formula of this GDD. Captures are taken **lit** (the chest lamp's beam on the trail) unless a criterion says dark. Every number is a registry constant, a Graph value cited by name, a worked value from this GDD's formulas, or a **[PLACEHOLDER]** default; every pass or fail value is stated in the criterion, never only behind a citation. Criteria that need the GA-T fields (`mode_steps`, `grow_steps`) set them in the fixture feed, so they run now against the resolver; the Graph-side half of GA-T is checked by the Graph's own criteria. Criterion ids are stable handles in one sequence (AC-1 to AC-188, with lettered splits); groups are by topic, so ids within a group are not contiguous. Criteria that wait on a neighbour that does not exist yet (the VS-B MIRROR path), and the points that are not testable as written, are in **Gaps / untestable as written** at the end. A *drawing row* means a row of the table of record (Detailed Design section 6) that draws at the descriptor's current `sev`. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the (auto-render) tag, the runner, the fixture root, the reviewer pool and the reference machine are new.)*

### A. Identity, statelessness and the truth channel

- **AC-1 Rebuild equivalence.** GIVEN a scripted run to Graph step N with 17 live and dead descriptors, WHEN the derived render state (stamp set, `size_idx`, extents, `dull_step`, `density_level`, frame indices at a fixed `present_time`) is dumped, the trail scene is torn down, and the scene is rebuilt from one `ALL` plus `present_time`, THEN the two dumps are equal field for field. (auto) [FT-1, FT-16; LS-20; P1]
- **AC-2 No state, no block, no hook.** GIVEN the source and scene tree, WHEN a static scan runs, THEN it finds no registered `block_id`, step hook or command, no light node, no `true_value()` call, no `Tween`, timer node, fade state, grow counter, last-seen flag or member that survives a Graph step other than pooled node references, the last poll and `diag_latch` (FT-1, FT-41), and no draw, reconcile or selection path reads `diag_latch`. (lint) [FT-1, FT-27, FT-41; PR-8; P1] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-3 Truth tag and read class.** GIVEN every node the system creates, WHEN the scene is walked, THEN each carries the TRUTH_PX grade of the Filter's PerceptionTag (registry: `perception_tag`), and the source reads no `PERCEPTION_OUT` view and calls no `true_value()`. (lint) [FT-2; LS-14; PR-10; P4]
- **AC-4 Bit-identical under the Filter.** GIVEN the Perception Filter at its maximum stage (violet bleed on pipe silhouettes) and a frost fan beside a pipe, WHEN the trail region is captured with the Filter pass on and off, THEN every trail pixel is bit-identical and only the pipe silhouette differs; the capture pair is retained in `production/qa/evidence/` (signing lead: art-director). (auto-render) [FT-2; PR-10, `PIPE:989`; P4] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-5 Filter-disabled path.** GIVEN the Filter's mask unavailable (it disables its own pass, `PIPE:735`), WHEN a frame is rendered, THEN the output equals the frame with the mask available and no code path branches on the Filter's state. (auto-render) [FT-2; PR-10; P4] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-6 No phantom trail (independent oracle).** GIVEN a class (a) phantom entity active at sanity stage 3 and the descriptor feed `fx_row_oracle.json`, WHEN the resolver's trail sources are counted, THEN the count equals the number of oracle rows marked "draws at this sev" in that file, which is **written by hand from the table of record and never generated from the resolver** (rows 6, 10, 22 and 23 never; 21 only at sev 4; 20 only at sev 2 or more; 24 and 25 by their own rules; 27 at sev 1 for every medium but GAS, which draws nothing); a second run at sanity stage 0 gives the identical set; and no node exists whose source descriptor is absent. (auto) [FT-2, FT-3, FT-39; LS-14; P4] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-7 Descriptor-only drawing.** GIVEN a derelict section with no authored fault, WHEN it is instantiated and rendered, THEN zero trail nodes exist; GIVEN one descriptor whose row draws at its `sev`, THEN exactly one trail source exists for it (a source may own several stamp nodes: a chain, a patch set, a puddle set). (auto) [FT-3; LS-14; P1]
- **AC-8 Anchor order.** GIVEN a `2 SHORT` and a `3 OVERHEAT` both with `subject_id` 41 on one anchor, WHEN rendered, THEN two trails draw in ascending (`subject_id`, `mode`, `medium`) order, each with its own stamp (a `2 SHORT` and a `3 OVERHEAT` are POINT rows and have no extent; two RUN descriptors would each have their own extent). (auto) [FT-3; P1] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review; re-review)*
- **AC-9 Forbidden elements.** GIVEN the scene and sheets, WHEN scanned, THEN no label, meter, bar, icon, outline, highlight, beacon, arrow or alarm-colour pixel is produced by the system, and nothing is parented to a crewmate or to a machine body sprite. (lint) [FT-4, FT-24; `LSG:1003`; P1]
- **AC-88 Sanity and class sweep.** GIVEN the same Graph state, WHEN the derived render state is dumped (auto) and the trail pixels are captured (auto-render) at sanity stages 0-3, under each hallucination class (a)-(d), and for two co-op players at different stages, THEN all dumps and all captures are identical. (auto, auto-render) [FT-2; knob invariant 8; P4] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-89 Upstream evidence and continuity.** GIVEN a trail on an edge, WHEN drawn at any extent, THEN stamps are contiguous (`start(i+1) = start(i) + STAMP_PX[idx_i]`), the thickest stamp is at the source, and no stamp lies on a node with no upstream evidence on the connecting edge. (auto) [FT-3; D.1; P1]
- **AC-91 No `intensity_pm` computed here.** GIVEN the source, WHEN scanned, THEN it contains no expression that produces an `intensity_pm` value; it only reads the field. (lint) [FT-30]
- **AC-92 Footprint kinds.** GIVEN `deposit_trace` of kind CHILL, BURN and STRESS that change no descriptor, THEN zero nodes are created; GIVEN 17 footprint writes in one step, THEN only the effects of the 16 the Graph accepted (`MAX_FOOTPRINTS_PER_STEP` 16) are drawn; GIVEN a GOUGE on a duct that yields only WORN, THEN nothing draws, and if it yields a LEAK the LEAK row draws. (auto, integration with the Graph) [FT-31]
- **AC-93 Read-only on the Graph.** GIVEN the source, WHEN scanned, THEN it calls only `read_view` queries (`failures`, `compartment`, `node`, `edge`) and no mutator (M1-M18, including M17 `deposit_trace`), and writes no `graph.state` field. (lint) [FT-1; LS-19; CR-9]

### B. Signal contract, size and extent (D.1, D.2, D.4)

The sev-1 case is **the seam weep** (`FX-SEAM` on a 288 px edge: GAS, no reach bound, a true onset from `built_step`, so it needs no GA-T) and, as a second sev-1 case, the `4 FREEZE` pre-stage; the sev-2 case is the `5 BREACH` pre-stage hairline, the sev-3 case the `1 LEAK/COOLANT` descriptor and the sev-4 case the `5 BREACH` bent rib. - **AC-17 Faster than walking, not faster than running.** GIVEN `GROW_PX_PER_STEP[4]`, WHEN checked against `V_WALK` 2 and `V_RUN` 3 px per tick (12 and 18 px per Graph step), THEN 12 < `GROW_PX_PER_STEP[4]` <= 18. ### C. Frost, cold, room surfaces and puddles (D.2b, D.2c, D.3)

- **AC-22 Frost reach values.** GIVEN `FROST_REACH_INTERP` true, WHEN `frost_reach` is evaluated at `T` -2000, 6000 and -20000 mC, THEN it returns 544, 192 and 998; with `FROST_REACH_INTERP` false it returns 960, 480 and 1152; a sweep of `T` from -45000 to 75000 mC is monotone non-increasing. - **AC-24c Wake capture.** GIVEN the same fixture, WHEN captured lit at wake, THEN, measured on the retained capture, the count of opaque trail pixels per 48 px segment along the run is non-increasing from the elbow segment to the far-wall segment, the far-wall segment is non-zero and the drip strip is on screen; AND each reviewer of pool RP-3 (a PT-1 participant among them) writes in free text what they see before any discussion, coded by two raters against the banned-word list of the Player Fantasy (marker, waypoint, highlight, indicator), with "coming from the elbow" as the expected reading (the PT-1 coding is kept beside the measurable half); the capture is retained in `production/qa/evidence/` and signed off by the art-director. (manual) [ET-1; `ART:142`; P1] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*
- **AC-25 Retreat with warmth.** GIVEN the cryo-bay trail at extent 336 and the heater warming 8 mC per Graph step, WHEN `T` reaches 3000 mC (step 625 after power-on), 6000 (step 1000) and 10000 (step 1500), THEN the frost part `frost_px` is 336, 192 and 0 (the head fan stamp alone at 10000 mC), the extent stays 336 and the rest of it is wet run (0, 3 and 5 wet pieces, D.2d); no state records the earlier extent. (auto) [FT-11; D.2, D.3; `LSG:839`]
- **AC-26a Coolant draw list.** GIVEN a `1 LEAK/COOLANT` descriptor in a COMFORT room, WHEN rendered, THEN its draw list holds a local frost stamp, a drip strip and, once the age bound reaches beyond the head stamp, the coolant wet-run pieces of D.2d, and zero mist frames. Retained in `production/qa/evidence/`, signed off by the art-director. (manual) [FT-11, FT-12; `ART:314`; P1] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-26c Coolant leak at COMFORT (user ruling D26, Option A).** GIVEN a `1 LEAK/COOLANT` descriptor, sev 3, on a 288 px (six-tile) edge in a COMFORT room (`T` 15000 mC), `grow_steps` 100, WHEN the resolver runs, THEN `trail_extent_px` is 288 (the age bound alone, D.2), `frost_px` is 0, and the draw list holds the m head fan stamp (96 px), four wet-run pieces at offsets 96, 144, 192 and 240 with widths 6, 4, 4 and 2 px, and a drip strip; the width is monotone non-increasing with distance from the source; GIVEN `grow_steps` 16, THEN no wet piece is drawn, and at `grow_steps` 17 (extent 102) the first piece (offset 96, width 6) is revealed 6 of 48 px; GIVEN the same descriptor at `T` -2000 mC with `grow_steps` 56 on a 336 px edge, THEN `frost_px` is 336 and no wet piece is drawn. - **AC-27 Room frost anchor and count.** GIVEN a FROST-band compartment with no leak, `T` -2000 mC, 42 tiles and `since_steps` 0 (a COMPARTMENT descriptor carries no age), WHEN `ATMO_COLD` renders, THEN patches grow from the room-fixed anchor tile (FT-11), `cover_pm` is 133, `m` is 5 and `n_patches` is 3; at `T` -15000 mC and colder `cover_pm` is 1000 and `n_patches` is 21 (`PATCH_STRIDE` 2, 42 tiles, `ceil`); at `T` -1 mC (the first FROST step) exactly one patch (the anchor tile's) draws; GIVEN a COOLANT descriptor appears or clears in the room at constant `T`, THEN the patch set is unchanged (AC-182). (auto) [D.2b; FT-19] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-149 Odd tile count.** GIVEN a room of 41 tiles at full coverage, THEN `n_patches` is 21 and the patch tiles are `c_0, c_2, ..., c_40`; GIVEN 43 tiles, 22 patches; the count always equals the number of selected tiles. (auto) [D.2b] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-150 Patch order is deterministic.** GIVEN a room whose tiles tie on Manhattan distance from the anchor tile, WHEN the candidates are sorted, THEN ties break by ascending row then ascending column, two clients produce an identical patch set, and the anchor tile is the room-fixed tile of FT-11 (the lowest `(row, col)` floor tile touching an exposed hull face, else the lowest `(row, col)` floor tile of the room), never a descriptor's anchor. (auto) [D.2b; FT-20] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-102 Puddle boundaries.** GIVEN `ml` 1, 499, 500, 1999, 2000 and 8000, THEN size is s, s, m, m, l, l and stamps are 1, 2, 2, 8, 8, 8. (auto) [D.2c]
- **AC-103 Frost knots.** GIVEN the knot edges 10000, 0, -15000, -40000 mC, THEN `frost_reach` is 0, 480, 960, 1152 (interpolated); `T` 70000 gives 0 and -45000 gives 1152; the intermediate product (at most 15000 x 480 = 7,200,000) stays within int32; with `FROST_REACH_INTERP` false the band edges give the step table. (auto) [D.3]
- **AC-104 Fuel in cold.** GIVEN a fuel puddle in a room at -20000 mC, THEN the puddle draws unfrozen and no coolant frost fan is drawn on the fuel stain. (auto) [FT-30; Edge Cases]
- **AC-182 Room frost is anchored to the room.** GIVEN a FROST-band compartment (42 tiles, `T` -2000 mC), WHEN a `1 LEAK/COOLANT` descriptor appears in it and then clears at constant `T`, THEN the patch set (tiles and variants) is bit-identical before, during and after; GIVEN two tiles that touch exposed hull faces, THEN the anchor tile is the one with the lower `(row, col)`; GIVEN none touches one, THEN it is the lowest `(row, col)` floor tile of the room. (auto) [FT-11; D.2b; row 20] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*

### D. Time, keys, restore and determinism (D.6, D.7)

- **AC-29 Handler does not draw.** GIVEN a notification with 38 keys, WHEN the handler returns, THEN it has stored the key set only and no scene node changed until the render callback. (auto) [FT-15; PR-8 rule 3; CR-10]
- **AC-30 Restore equivalence.** GIVEN a save taken mid-cascade, WHEN it is restored, THEN `graph.state` `state_hash` is equal in both runs AND the derived render state (stamp set, sizes, extents, dull steps) equals the uninterrupted run at the same step after one `ALL`. GIVEN the player dies and the game restarts from the last autosave, THEN the same holds and no "seen" flag or first-sight state exists. (auto) [FT-16; LS-1; SL-17; P1; binding user decision]
- **AC-31 One-frame reconcile.** GIVEN an `ALL` after a 300-node breach and an injected stub cost source that reports 1.2 ms for the full re-read (no wall clock is read), WHEN the next render callback runs, THEN the full re-read is reconciled against the pool within that frame. Run twice: with the shipped `TRAIL_RECONCILE_BUDGET_MS` 4 and with it set to 0.5; THEN the log line count for the same fixture is 0 and 1 respectively (one line on the rising edge, FT-41), no frame is skipped, no trail deferred, and the resolver output is identical in both. (auto) [FT-16, FT-41; `KERN:466`; CR-10] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-32 No new block.** GIVEN the kernel's block registry after boot, WHEN the system is enabled, THEN no block, hook or command id belongs to it. (auto) [FT-1; CR-3, CR-8; SL-17, SL-25]
- **AC-33 Dormant and wake.** GIVEN a dormant section with a fault, WHEN rendered, THEN it emits no trail; WHEN it wakes, THEN the trail appears at the `grow_steps` and `mode_steps` the section ran (the Graph's M19 advanced both onsets by the dormant interval), with no catch-up animation. (auto, integration with the Graph) [FT-19; `LSG:822`; LS-17] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*
- **AC-34 Pause.** GIVEN the kernel PAUSED, WHEN 5 s of wall time pass, THEN extent, frame index and all animation are unchanged. (auto) [FT-1; PR-9]

- **AC-124 No particles, no clock.** GIVEN the source and scene, THEN no particle node exists while the particle spike flag is unset, the animation frame is computed only from `present_time`, and no wall-clock call or tick counter is read. (lint) [FT-21; PR-8 rule 6]
- **AC-125 Forbidden colours and noise.** GIVEN all sheets, THEN no pixel equals Alarm Red `#E0102A`, Chemlight `#86B896` or a Tungsten light colour; every feature is at least 2 x 2 px except the hairline composite; no isolated single pixel. (lint) [FT-22]
- **AC-126 Dither cover.** GIVEN `cover16` 4, 8, 12, 16, THEN every 4 x 4 block of a captured mist has exactly that many lit 2 x 2 cells, overlapped stamps agree, and the pattern is identical on two clients. (auto-render) [FT-23; D.4] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-127 No distortion shader.** GIVEN the materials, THEN none reads the screen texture or displaces UVs. (lint) [FT-24]
- **AC-128 Containment at doors.** GIVEN a floor run reaching a doorway sill, THEN it stops at the sill; GIVEN a wall fitting within 72 px of a door or cap seam, THEN the wall part is cropped to its host face. (auto) [FT-13, FT-37]
- **AC-129 Reveal terminal.** GIVEN extent equal to the end of the last stamp, THEN `thr` = 127 and the terminal piece replaces the tip. (auto) [D.10]
- **AC-130 Actors over marks.** GIVEN an actor or object standing on a floor trail, THEN the mark is drawn beneath it (z +1) and the descriptor and acoustic class are unchanged. (auto) [FT-25]
- **AC-132 Resize.** GIVEN the window is resized at run time, THEN `k_auto` and the field change, the next frame re-evaluates which anchors are inside the field from the poll cache, and no key and no stored state is involved. (auto) [FT-34]

### H. Never on a body; light, glow and exposure

- **AC-57a Anchored to the fitting.** GIVEN a `3 OVERHEAT` descriptor on a machine, WHEN a class (b) hallucination redraws the body, THEN no trail node is parented to or positioned by the body sprite and the scorch and shimmer remain at the anchor port. (auto) [FT-24; LS-14; P4]
- **AC-57b The floor disagrees.** GIVEN a scrubber rendered whole with a green gauge (class (b)) and a leaking outlet fitting, WHEN captured lit, THEN the mist at the outlet is as at full sanity; signed off by the art-director, retained in `production/qa/evidence/`. (manual) [FT-24; ET-5; P4]
- **AC-58 Dark room.** GIVEN a room with the lamp off and no fixture, WHEN captured and compared per pixel with the same frame with the trail layer hidden, THEN no non-emissive trail pixel has a luminance above the hull-floor tint (Void Steel `#0B1018` tinted, L\* about 5, `max(albedo x ambient, floor tint)`), the descriptor and its acoustic class exist, and any live emissive element is visible and listed. - **AC-59 No light written.** GIVEN the source, WHEN scanned, THEN it creates no light node, registers no emitter and writes no exposure value. (lint) [FT-27; EM-17; P3]
- **AC-60 Glow table complete and in bounds.** GIVEN the glow table, WHEN checked, THEN every row has `key`, a palette hex, `radius_px` <= 72 (blank inherits `FAULT_GLOW_REACH_PX` 72) and a flicker class bounded by FT-29; rows exist only for `2`, `7`, `3`, `9`, `1/FUEL` and `1/GAS`; and no row exists for frost, hull, puddle or strain descriptors. (lint) [FT-27; EM-17, `EM:333`; P3]
- **AC-61 Derived glow.** GIVEN a `2 SHORT` at sev 3 and the glow table, WHEN Emitter State's `emitter.place` runs on the Graph tick of the descriptor, THEN one FAULT_GLOW record exists on that Graph tick and never later (`EM:200`), the trail's emissive layer adds nothing to the exposure query, and with eleven FAULT_GLOW candidates, none protected (each older than `W_MIN_WARNING_S`), `MAX_FAULT_GLOW` (10) keeps the ten with the highest `sev`, ties by ascending descriptor id (EM-17). (auto, integration with Emitter State) [FT-27; EM-17; P3]
- **AC-62 Cyan gating.** GIVEN a `1 LEAK/GAS` descriptor with `intensity_pm` above 0 and then 0, WHEN Emitter State's `emitter.place` runs on the Graph tick of the `intensity_pm` change, THEN a FAULT_GLOW record exists while flow is positive and is removed on the tick it reaches 0 (`EM:549`, `EM:678`). (auto, integration with Emitter State) [FT-18, FT-27; EM-17; P3]
- **AC-138 Instruments witness.** GIVEN each row of the Graph's LS-22 table, THEN a drawn trail witness exists when lit and shares no node with the gauge. (auto) [Interactions table #13; In-World Instruments IW-12] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-140 Lighting handover.** GIVEN each of the six glow rows of V/A.6, WHEN the emissive layer's peak and held luminance are read through the exposure seam that 2D Lighting's harness uses (LO-14, LO-24), THEN each equals the V/A.6 value (`Y_PEAK_PM` 577 for Power Amber; the held level is `ARC_HELD_GLOW_PM` 600 permille of peak) to within 1 of 255 in 8-bit luminance, and `flash_count` runs on them without error and reports F <= 3 for the AC-64 fixture. (auto-render) [Interactions table #8; D.8b] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: it had no pass condition)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-90 Attention is invisible.** GIVEN the Attention system at its minimum and maximum, THEN trail captures are identical. (auto-render) [FT-4; P3] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

### I. Accessibility and the flash clamp (D.8, D.8b)

- **AC-63a Reduced motion state.** GIVEN reduced motion on, WHEN a 60 s run is made, THEN `graph.state` `state_hash` is equal to the run with it off, the derived render state is equal except the moving-medium frame index (frame 0), and no trail, hairline or audio is suppressed. (auto) [FT-28; PR-14, `PIPE:232`, `PIPE:847`]
- **AC-63b Hold frames.** GIVEN reduced motion on, THEN every moving strip shows its frame 0. (auto) [FT-28]
- **AC-63c Hold-pose identification.** GIVEN the hold poses, WHEN viewed as stills, THEN each is identified by its behaviour word per the V/A.5 table (mist = peak-coverage frame with one wisp leaning along `dir`; drip = hanging bead with a 2 px streak below; vent debris = mid-flight cluster; AIRFLOW dust = directional streaks with a tapered tail; arc = steady glow at 60 percent of peak with the four static spark-tip pixels lit, no pops, and told from a Tungsten fixture lamp and from a `7 OVERLOAD` rim in a three-way still (V/A.11 item 14, AC-187); shimmer = static three-line wave); signed off by the art-director, retained in `production/qa/evidence/`. (manual) [FT-28; `ART` 4.7]
- **AC-64 Flash clamp (capture).** GIVEN two l arcs in one compartment with `ARC_HOLD_FRAMES` 3 and `ARC_SWING_PM` 240, WHEN `flash_count` runs on a 60 s capture with reduce flashing set OFF explicitly (it is ON by default at first launch, FT-36) and glow on, THEN F <= `FLASH_MAX_PER_S` 3 for each light, for the region and for the frame mean; each arc runs at 2 flashes per second whatever the arc count (the rate is not divided), the swing is 86 each, and the regional luminance change is below 10 percent in every 1 s window. Pass threshold: F <= 3 in every 1 s window (spike S5). (spike) [FT-29; D.8b; PR-13, `PIPE:228`] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-65 Reduce flashing.** GIVEN reduce flashing on (the first-launch default, FT-36), WHEN an arc runs, THEN there are zero luminance transitions of 10 percent or more, the glow is held at `ARC_HELD_GLOW_PM` (600 permille of peak), and the scorch still draws. (auto) [FT-29; D.8b] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-66 Animation frame.** GIVEN `present_time` 5.40 s, 12 fps, phase 2, mist `N_FRAMES` 6, WHEN `anim_frame` runs, THEN frame 0; under reduced motion 0; a growth tip at extent 336 has `tip_frame` 1. (auto) [FT-21, FT-28; D.8]
- **AC-67 Settings are read only.** GIVEN the settings toggles, WHEN the source is scanned, THEN it reads them and writes none. (lint) [FT-36]
- **AC-131 Both toggles.** GIVEN reduced motion and reduce flashing both on, THEN the arc is a steady glow on its hold frame; scorch, extent and dullness are unchanged. (auto) [FT-28, FT-29]
- **AC-173 One arc hold level.** GIVEN an arc under reduced motion (hold frame 0), under reduce flashing, and under both, WHEN its emissive level is read, THEN all three equal `ARC_HELD_GLOW_PM` (600 permille of peak); GIVEN the source, WHEN scanned, THEN no second hold level exists (the expression `ARC_SWING_PM / 2` is absent). (auto, lint) [FT-28, FT-29; D.8b] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-178 Flash counting unit.** GIVEN windows with 4, 5, 6 and 7 opposing transitions, WHEN `flash_count` is evaluated with the ruled unit `ceil(n_transitions / 2)`, THEN F is 2, 3, 3 and 4 (F <= `FLASH_MAX_PER_S` 3 holds for 4, 5 and 6 transitions and fails at 7); GIVEN `TRAIL_ANIM_FPS` 12 and `ARC_HOLD_FRAMES` 3, THEN the derived `ARC_FLICKER_PER_S` is 2; GIVEN 9 fps and hold 3 (3 transitions), THEN it is 2, not 1. (auto, lint) [D.8b; FT-29; registry `flash_count`; user ruling D91 ii] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*
- **AC-135 Arc knob lint.** GIVEN (`TRAIL_ANIM_FPS`, `ARC_HOLD_FRAMES`) of (24, 3) (`F_win` 5), (12, 2) (`F_win` 4; hold 2 is also below the range minimum of 3) and (18, 3) (`F_win` 4), THEN the knob lint **rejects** each (it never clamps); (12, 3) (`F_win` 3), (15, 3) and (8, 3) pass; `ARC_HOLD_FRAMES` 1 and 2 are rejected; a configuration that sets `ARC_FLICKER_PER_S` directly is rejected (it is derived and read-only); `ARC_SWING_PM` 241 is out of its 0-240 range and rejected; knob invariant 10 (the D.8b swing bound) is checked for every n in 1-3 and every emissive size; a compartment with more live-capable SHORT anchors than `ARC_PER_REGION_MAX` raises a content lint. - **AC-155 One arc.** GIVEN one live l arc in a compartment (`n_arcs_room` 1), THEN `swing_i_pm` is 173, the regional swing at `REGION_MIN_PX` 9,216 is 8.98 percent (< 9) and the rate is 2 flashes per second; for one m arc `swing_i_pm` is 240 (bound 389) and for one s arc 240. - **AC-156 Two arcs.** GIVEN two live l arcs within one neighbourhood window (D.8b), THEN `swing_i_pm` is 86 each, the regional swing is 8.93 percent (< 9) and the rate is still 2 flashes per second each; for two m arcs 194 each (8.95 percent). (auto) [D.8b] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

### J. Footprints, patches and the pre-stage

- **AC-69a Footprint trail grammar.** GIVEN a RUPTURE and a GOUGE through `deposit_trace` on a hull plate that falls below `LEAK_HP` 4000, THEN the trail is the ordinary BREACH or LEAK mark along the rib or seam with no variant by `cause`. (auto, integration with the Graph) [FT-31; `ART:565`; LS-18]
- **AC-69b No radial variant asset.** GIVEN the sheet list, THEN no radial-variant asset exists. (lint) [FT-31]
- **AC-70 A patch hands the LEAK to the weep.** GIVEN a frosted coolant pipe (`FX-CRYO`), WHEN a patch lands (`PATCH_CAP_BP` 6000 is above `LEAK_HP` 4000) and the Graph emits `27 PATCH_WEEP/COOLANT` in place of the LEAK on the same step, THEN on the next frame the fan, the wet run and the drip are gone, exactly one s-trace frost-spur stamp (row 27, `mode_steps` 0, DRAWN_STATIC) is drawn at the fitting, no frame shows both and none shows neither, the pipe carries no WORN trail node, no alpha is below 255 at any frame, and the room's `ATMO_COLD` crust (sev 2 or more) remains while the band is FROST or colder. (auto) [FT-32; LS-11, LS-23; P1] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*
- **AC-70b Row 27 per medium.** GIVEN `27 PATCH_WEEP` descriptors (sev 1, `intensity_pm` 0) of medium COOLANT, FUEL, ELECTRIC, STRUCT and GAS, WHEN the resolver runs, THEN they resolve to the s-trace frost spur, the s-trace puddle blob, the s-trace scorch, the s-trace hairline run (<= 16 px) and nothing (DRAWN_STATIC and empty) respectively; each non-GAS draw has no extent, no accumulation stamp, no drip and no glow request, the GAS case writes no pixel while its descriptor and its HISS class still exist, and a WORN descriptor beside any of them draws nothing; the descriptor is never silenced by its `intensity_pm` 0. The earlier switch-aware branch is deleted: the Graph adopted the weep. (auto) [FT-32, FT-18; row 27; LS-14] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*
- **AC-179 The three-way contrast and the weep's dullness.** GIVEN the same coolant elbow in three Graph states, WHEN the resolver runs, THEN a closed valve (LEAK, `intensity_pm` 0, `grow_steps` 50) resolves to the full still fan at its frozen extent, a patch (`27 PATCH_WEEP/COOLANT`) to one s-trace spur with no extent and no drip, and a cap (no descriptor) to nothing; GIVEN a `27 PATCH_WEEP` present at `mode_steps` 0, 4000 and 15000, THEN `dull_step_shown` is 0, 1 and 2 (the ongoing-row cap of FT-17, never 3); GIVEN the patch decays and the pipe re-leaks, THEN the weep despawns and the LEAK spawns at `grow_steps` 0 on the same frame with no frame showing both. (auto) [FT-32, FT-17; D.5] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*
- **AC-71 Pre-stage hairline (regression guard).** GIVEN a hull plate at `hp` 6500 with the mode-5 STRUCT descriptor at sev 1 (confirmed by the Graph, `LSG:170`), WHEN rendered, THEN an s hairline draws and the TICK acoustic class is assigned; GIVEN a regression in which the Graph emits WORN instead, THEN nothing draws, the diagnostic fires and the build-time lint "every id has a row" reports it; and GIVEN a regression in which a patched non-hull subject emits WORN instead of `27 PATCH_WEEP`, THEN the hand-written oracle row for id 27 in `fx_row_oracle.json` fails. (auto) [FT-32; `LSG:127`; P2] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-144 Flow-zero hull pre-stage cue at k = 1.** GIVEN a hull plate at `hp` below `HP_OK` with a mode-5 STRUCT pre-stage descriptor at sev 1 or 2 and `intensity_pm` 0, WHEN the camera is at k = 1 and the 2 px hairline is not resolved, THEN the descriptor carries the TICK acoustic class (assigned by the Graph, `LSG:170`) and Adaptive Audio plays it at the descriptor's anchor (AC-AA-117 discharges the audio side), and no marker, icon or additional layer is drawn for it; the cue is on the consequence channel, so it is identical with the Perception Filter at any stage. (auto at the descriptor and acoustic-request level; the audible result is Adaptive Audio's criterion AC-AA-117 and is not repeated here, so this criterion has no subjective half) [FT-34, FT-35; LS-14, `LSG:215`; P2, P4] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

### L. Budget, performance and cross-document consistency (D.9)

- **AC-80 Draw-call count at every k (host-independent).** GIVEN `fx_mvp_worst` rendered on the `ft-render` job, WHEN the renderer's draw-call counter is read per viewport at every ladder step of 1080p (1-5), 1440p (1-6) and 2160p (1-8), THEN `D_FT` <= 38 (<= 60 under the Filter's silhouette mask route) at every one; the count depends on pages, rows and materials and not on machine speed, so this criterion names no reference machine, and it holds only as far as spike S2's per-instance route does (D.9). (auto-render) [FT-34; D.9; PR D.16] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: split from the machine-dependent half, AC-188; specialist disagreement resolved for the performance-analyst)*
- **AC-81 Stamp cap.** GIVEN `fx_mvp_worst` at 2160p k = 1, WHEN stamps are counted, THEN `stamps_world` is 661 and <= `TRAIL_MAX_STAMPS` 704; `fx_design_max` gives 618; exceeding 704 logs one line per crossing and never culls; `TRAIL_MAX_STAMPS` equals `64 x ceil(stamps_at_caps / 64)` with `stamps_at_caps` summed from the class caps of D.9 (661) and not read from a fixture. (auto) [FT-19; D.9] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-82 Field table.** GIVEN the Pipeline's `zoom_ladder`, WHEN the field and tile-areas (floor division of the field area by 48 squared) are computed at the three reference displays, THEN they equal: 1080p, k 1-5: 1920 x 1080 (900), 960 x 540 (225), 640 x 360 (100), 480 x 270 (56), 384 x 216 (36); 1440p, k 1-6: 2560 x 1440 (1600), 1280 x 720 (400), 853 x 480 (177), 640 x 360 (100), 512 x 288 (64), 426 x 240 (44); 2160p, k 1-8: 3840 x 2160 (3600), 1920 x 1080 (900), 1280 x 720 (400), 960 x 540 (225), 768 x 432 (144), 640 x 360 (100), 548 x 308 (73), 480 x 270 (56). (auto) [FT-34; D.9; PR C6]
- **AC-83 Idle-frame cost.** GIVEN `fx_steady`, WHEN idle render frames (no step, no key, no frame-index change, no camera-field change) are profiled on `REFERENCE_MACHINE` under the measurement protocol of the preamble, THEN the system costs <= `TRAIL_FRAME_MS_P99` 0.25 ms at p99 (nearest rank, at least 100 idle samples). Pass threshold 0.25 ms; spike S5. (spike) [FT-15, FT-41; D.7] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-84 First-use hitch.** GIVEN the first leak of a session on `REFERENCE_MACHINE` with `fx_steady`, WHEN its first frame is timed, THEN it is <= 1.5 x the median frame time of the preceding 600 frames, in each of 10 cold launches (every launch must pass). Pass threshold 1.5 x; spike S4. (spike) [FT-21, FT-41] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-174 Step-frame cost.** GIVEN `fx_steady` and then `fx_mvp_worst`, WHEN step frames (the frame that polls and applies keys, excluding `ALL`) are profiled on `REFERENCE_MACHINE`, THEN the system costs <= `TRAIL_STEP_FRAME_MS_P99` 1.5 ms at p99 (nearest rank over at least 600 step frames) in both. Pass threshold 1.5 ms [PLACEHOLDER]; spike S5. (spike) [FT-15, FT-41; D.7] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-183 Four frame classes under a walking player.** GIVEN `fx_mvp_worst` and a scripted player walking at `V_WALK` across the loaded world for at least 600 Graph steps (about 3,600 render frames), WHEN every render frame is classed (idle, pan, animation flip, step) and timed on `REFERENCE_MACHINE`, THEN the nearest-rank p99 of each class is within its budget: idle `TRAIL_FRAME_MS_P99` 0.25 ms, pan `TRAIL_PAN_FRAME_MS_P99` 0.5 ms, animation flip `TRAIL_ANIM_FRAME_MS_P99` 0.75 ms (0.25 ms if spike S2 made the frame index shader-driven) and step 1.5 ms; every class has at least 100 samples and the evidence file carries a host header. (spike) [FT-15, FT-41; D.7] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*
- **AC-188 Render-structure spike (S2).** GIVEN the S2 prototype on `REFERENCE_MACHINE`, WHEN it is run, THEN three separate pass lines are reported: (a) `CanvasItem` shaders receive per-instance data in 4.7.2 (yes or no); (b) the four packed values (reveal threshold, dull step, coverage rank, clock) read back equal to the value written for all 256 levels of every channel under `hdr_2d`; (c) 64 distinct parameter sets draw in one batch with no draw split; and the measured `L_sets` and `I_wall` are recorded with a host header; if (a) fails, `D_FT_MAX` is re-derived at the ADR (D.9). Recorded in `docs/engine-reference/godot/` and the ADR before the first render story starts. (spike) [FT-21, FT-34; D.9; V/A.13] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*
- **AC-160 Idle frame writes nothing.** GIVEN a frame with no new `step_no`, no keys, no frame-index change and no camera change, WHEN scene writes and resolver calls are counted, THEN both are zero. (auto) [FT-41] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-161 Material warm-up.** GIVEN the load sequence, WHEN the first frame is drawn, THEN every material and shader variant of the table of record has already been instantiated by the warm-up draw (a counter equals the variant count) and the first leak's frame carries no compile event. - **AC-162 Pool pre-size.** GIVEN the load sequence, THEN the node pool holds `TRAIL_MAX_STAMPS` nodes before the first descriptor resolves; GIVEN 705 stamps, THEN the pool grows, one line is logged and no stamp is culled. (auto) [FT-41; D.9] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-163 Pixel-neutral reducers.** GIVEN `fx_mvp_worst` rendered with the FT-41 reducers (write-only-changed, retained draw lists, per-room batching) on and off, WHEN the two renders are compared, THEN they are bit-identical and the reducers-on render performs fewer scene writes. The criteria that formerly waited on Adaptive Audio, In-World Instruments, Interaction, Lighting, Derelict Authoring, Horror Entities, Attention and Layout have moved into their groups now that those GDDs are designed (AC-137, AC-138, AC-139, AC-140, AC-44, AC-79, AC-90, AC-144, AC-55, AC-143). None blocks authoring; each is also in Open Questions.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

- **AC-72 MIRROR parity (VS-B; auto against a mocked summary, not an MVP gate).** GIVEN a mocked `SHARED_SUMMARY` carrying `(id, mode, sev, alarm_eligible, intensity bucket, since_steps, mode_steps, grow_steps)` (`LSG:210`) fed through the descriptor-feed seam, which is testable now, before the Co-op GDD exists, THEN a MIRROR client's derived render state (stamp set, extents, dull steps, dominance computed from the `(mode, sev)` pairs) equals the HOST's for the same descriptors; there is no legacy branch to test. (auto against the mock; excluded from MVP gating, the real transport waits for Co-op) [FT-33; LS-21; CR-10; P1] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: specialist disagreement resolved, the mock seam is adopted and the gate stays out of MVP)*
- **AC-73 Different ladder steps (VS-B).** GIVEN two clients at different k, THEN their base-px trail sets are equal wherever both fields overlap. [FT-34; PR C6]
- **Blocked at integration, owed by a neighbour** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: ET-4 (the verdict), AC-43, AC-44 and AC-171 cannot be met against the real content until Derelict Authoring's DA-17 and its lint seed `mode_since_step` and `mode_key` (Open Questions 25); the resolver halves run now against fixtures. The Onboarding half of AC-24a/AC-180 likewise waits for the Onboarding fixture to seed `mode_key` (Open Questions 24).
- **Not testable as written (routed, not criteria):** (1) the hairline's 2 px composite against the art bible's minimum-feature checklist is an amendment candidate, a routing note; (2) the reviewer-pool thresholds of RP-3 (3 of 3 for m and l, 2 of 3 for s-tier and k = 1) are the review's proposal and are confirmed or retuned at the first review; (3) hue fidelity under the alarm sweep is a Lighting spike item (greyscale polarity and form carry the read, AC-77a and AC-77b); (4) the playtest targets in Game Feel are placeholders until a first playtest; (5) the pre-re-leak tell for a patched subject is the Graph's `27 PATCH_WEEP`, closed (AC-70, AC-70b, AC-179; Open Questions 4); the hull pre-stage is confirmed by the Graph and guarded by AC-71; at k = 1 the hull-fatigue cue is the TICK acoustic class, AC-144; (6) a GOUGE on a duct surfacing only as WORN is a known truth-channel hole, tested only as "nothing draws" (AC-92). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

## Open Questions

*Owner: **user** (a taste, scope or product decision only the user can make), **agent** (a specialist or a later authoring stage can close it), or **GDD: name** (a neighbouring GDD or its owner closes it; this GDD edits none of those files). Target: **playtest**, **next GDD** (the neighbour's authoring or a consistency pass), or **ADR** (`/architecture-decision`). Every item is non-blocking for this GDD unless it says "blocking". User-decision items come first; each has a stated default the GDD already carries.*

### Decisions for the user (defaults in force; 6a-6e ruled 2026-10-27)

| 1 | **Dulling granularity: `DULL_STEPS` 4 or 8?** 4 is the default (CP-8; the technical-artist's and systems-designer's placeholder, a 16-cell dither of fresh-hue cells {16, 11, 5, 0}); the art-director's alternative is 8 (125 permille per step, a finer age read, a scan matrix twice as large). A stop is reached every 6.7 minutes at 4 and 2.9 at 8 (`DRIED_AFTER_STEPS` 12000; the last, dried stop begins at `DRIED_AFTER_STEPS` itself, D.5). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | user | playtest |

No requirement asks for the stricter reading, so 2 stands; the criterion checks the 28.8 s ceiling. | user | playtest |

The Graph restamps `mode_since_step` on every change of `mode_key` = `(mode << 3) \| sev`, which has three consequences the re-review joined: (1) a worsening fault visibly shrinks (a sev 3 trail of 288 px restarts as a 48 px head), which can read as healing, and a slow 1-2-3 ramp inside the 100-step fairness window never crosses 288 px, so the FT-9 tell window is not guaranteed there; (2) POINT-row accumulation (`acc_step`, `ACC_STEP_LEN` 300) restarts as the cable worsens and never advances inside the 10 s window; (3) a valve reopen is a one-frame extent step. **Preferred option, for the Graph owner and the user:** a rise-only restart for sev 4 only (ET-3's racing head), `extent = max(new head, prior edge-limited age)` on rises 1 to 3, no restart on falls, `mode_since_step` shifted forward by the isolated interval on reopen, and `acc_step` keyed on the mode age. Tests to add if adopted: a sev 1 -> 2 -> 3 ramp within 100 steps (no shrink; the crossing guarantee holds; `acc_step` non-decreasing). **Not changed here:** the default (D28) is a pending-confirmation default and the restamp is Graph-owned, so the restart rule and `ACC_STEP_LEN` 300 stand; the re-review's suggestion to shorten `ACC_STEP_LEN` is declined for that reason (R10). | user (with the Graph owner) | confirm at the next review |
